# Prompt A/B Testing Harness for Grounding Failures

## Context

The full grounding pipeline scores 9/15 exact code match (60%) and has 3 relation mismatches against our golden test data. The 9 failures break into two categories:

- **6 code mismatches** — MedGemma selects a wrong CUI (subspecific variant, alternate version, or close synonym)
- **3 relation mismatches** — Field mapper generates wrong operator (inverted, wrong class, or missed negation)

We need a test harness that runs **only the failing entities** with **multiple prompt variants** and produces a comparison report showing which variants fix which failures.

---

## Files to Create

### 1. `tests/e2e/prompt_variants.py` — Variant definitions (pure data)

Defines all prompt variant content as dicts. Each variant has:
- `name`: short label
- `grounding_system`: replacement system prompt (or `None` for baseline)
- `grounding_evaluate`: replacement Jinja2 evaluate template (or `None`)
- `field_mapper_rules_extra`: extra rules to append to field mapper prompt (or `None`)

**MedGemma Variants** (for code selection — 4 variants):

| Variant | Name | Changes |
|---------|------|---------|
| A | `baseline` | No changes — current production prompts |
| B | `specificity_guard` | Add 2 rules to `grounding_system.jinja2`: (1) prefer parent concept over subspecific variants when entity text has no qualifier, (2) prefer primary CUI over alternates when preferred_terms are near-identical |
| C | `context_emphasis` | Modify `grounding_evaluate.jinja2` instructions: add cross-check step — if candidate adds a qualifier not in entity text (e.g. "Juvenile", "Ectopic"), it's too specific; prefer unqualified parent |
| D | `combined` | Both B and C together |

**Field Mapper Variants** (for relation extraction — 2 variants):

| Variant | Name | Changes |
|---------|------|---------|
| A | `baseline` | No changes |
| B | `operator_rules` | Append 3 rules: (1) extract comparison operators verbatim from text, never invert; (2) `within` only for numeric min/max ranges, not discrete categorical lists; (3) negation patterns (`non-`, `not `, `no `) map to `relation='!='` |

### 2. `tests/e2e/run_prompt_variants.py` — Main test harness

**Failing entities to test** (hardcoded list):

Code mismatches (tested with MedGemma variants A-D):
| Snippet | Entity | Expected Code |
|---------|--------|---------------|
| 0 | Serum creatinine | C0201975 |
| 1 | eGFR | C0858118 |
| 3:0 | Ankylosing Spondylitis | C0038013 |
| 3:1 | Radiologic examination | C0034693 |
| 4:3 | GBA gene mutation | C4225361 |
| 8:0 | Pregnancy | C0032961 |

Relation mismatches (tested with field mapper variants A-B):
| Snippet | Entity | Expected Relation |
|---------|--------|-------------------|
| 2:1 | Body Mass Index | `>` |
| 5:0 | ASA physical status | `=` |
| 8:0 | Pregnancy | `!=` |

**Monkey-patching strategy:**
- MedGemma templates: `unittest.mock.patch("protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider._render_template")` with a function that intercepts template names and returns variant content (or falls through to original)
- Field mapper prompt: `unittest.mock.patch("protocol_processor.nodes.ground.generate_field_mappings")` with a wrapper that appends extra rules to the prompt before calling the LLM

**Execution flow:**
1. Load `test_snippets.json`, extract only failing entities
2. For each MedGemma variant (A-D): patch templates, run 6 code-mismatch entities through `_ground_entity_parallel`, record `selected_code`
3. For each field mapper variant (A-B): patch field mapper, run 3 relation-mismatch entities, record `relation` from first field mapping
4. Print comparison report tables
5. Optionally save JSON results to `tests/e2e/prompt_variant_results/`

**API call budget:** 6 entities x 4 variants + 3 entities x 2 variants = ~30 grounding calls total

**Report output format:**
```
CODE SELECTION VARIANTS (MedGemma prompt changes)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Entity                    Expected    baseline  specificity  context  combined
Serum creatinine          C0201975    C0201976  ...          ...      ...
eGFR                      C0858118    C3811844  ...          ...      ...
...
Totals:                               0/6       ?/6          ?/6      ?/6

RELATION EXTRACTION VARIANTS (Field mapper prompt changes)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
Entity                    Expected    baseline  operator_rules
Body Mass Index           >           <         ...
ASA physical status       =           within    ...
Pregnancy                 !=          =         ...
Totals:                               0/3       ?/3
```

---

None. All production code stays unchanged. The harness uses monkey-patching to inject variant prompts at runtime.

4. Run full harness: `set -a && source .env && set +a && uv run python tests/e2e/run_prompt_variants.py`
5. Analyze report, identify winning variant(s)

```
    49→class AgenticReasoningResult(BaseModel):
    50→    """MedGemma's 3-question reasoning output for failed grounding retry.
    51→
    52→    Used by agentic_reasoning_loop to determine whether to skip an entity,
    53→    apply derived entity mapping, or rephrase the query for a better search.
    54→
    55→    Attributes:
    56→        should_skip: True if entity is not a valid medical criterion.
    57→        is_derived: True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept.
    58→        derived_term: Standard concept term if is_derived is True.
    59→        rephrased_query: Rephrased medical terminology query if applicable.
    60→        gemini_suggestion: Optional additional reformulation from Gemini.
    61→        reasoning: Brief explanation of the reasoning decisions.
    62→    """
    63→
    64→    should_skip: bool = Field(
    65→        default=False,
    66→        description=(
    67→            "True if entity is not a valid medical criterion "
    68→            "(e.g., consent, participation, willingness)"
    69→        ),
    70→    )
    71→    is_derived: bool = Field(
    72→        default=False,
    73→        description="True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept",
    74→    )

    76→        default=None,
    77→        description=(
    78→            "Standard concept term if is_derived is True "
    79→            "(e.g., 'age' for 'age >= 18 years')"

    84→        description=(

   133→def _get_medgemma_model() -> Any:
   134→    """Get or create MedGemma model instance."""
   135→    global _model_loader  # noqa: PLW0603
   136→    if _model_loader is None:
   137→        from inference.config import AgentConfig
   138→        from inference.model_garden import create_model_loader
   139→

   141→    return _model_loader()

   144→async def _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> GroundingDecision:
   145→    """Structure raw MedGemma output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   146→
   147→    Args:
   148→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma output (free-form medical reasoning).
   149→
   150→    Returns:
   151→        GroundingDecision with selected code and confidence.
   152→    """
   153→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   154→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   155→
   156→    if not google_api_key:
   157→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   158→
   159→    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   160→
   161→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   162→        model=gemini_model_name,
   163→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   164→    )
   165→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(GroundingDecision)
   166→
   167→    prompt = (
   168→        "Extract the grounding decision from this medical terminology analysis."
   169→        " Return the selected code, system, preferred term, confidence,"
   170→        f" and reasoning.\n\n{raw_text}"
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    with llm_span("gemini_structure_decision", gemini_model_name) as llm:
   174→        llm.set_request(prompt)
   175→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   176→        resp_text = str(result) if not isinstance(result, dict) else str(result)
   177→        llm.set_response(resp_text)
   178→
   179→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   180→        return GroundingDecision.model_validate(result)
   181→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   182→
   183→
   184→async def medgemma_decide(
   185→    entity: dict,
   186→    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate],
   187→    criterion_context: str,
   188→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
   189→    """Use MedGemma to select the best terminology match for an entity.
   190→
   191→    MedGemma evaluates the candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and
   192→    selects the most appropriate code for the entity. Returns a result with
   193→    confidence=0.0 and no code if no candidates are available or none are
   194→    appropriate.
   195→
   196→    Args:
   197→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   198→        candidates: List of GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   199→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   200→
   201→    Returns:
   202→        EntityGroundingResult with the selected code, confidence, and reasoning.
   203→    """
   204→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   205→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   206→    criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   207→
   208→    # If no candidates, return result with confidence=0.0
   209→    if not candidates:
   210→        logger.info(
   211→            "No candidates for entity '%s' (type=%s, criterion=%s)"
   212→            " — returning empty grounding",
   213→            entity_text[:50],
   214→            entity_type,
   215→            criterion_id[:12],
   216→        )
   217→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   218→            entity_text=entity_text,
   219→            entity_type=entity_type,
   220→            selected_code=None,
   221→            selected_system=None,
   222→            preferred_term=None,
   223→            confidence=0.0,
   224→            candidates=[],
   225→            reasoning="No terminology candidates available from any API",
   226→        )
   227→
   228→    try:
   229→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   230→
   231→        system_prompt = _render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   232→        evaluate_prompt = _render_template(
   233→            "grounding_evaluate.jinja2",
   234→            entity_text=entity_text,
   235→            entity_type=entity_type,
   236→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   237→            candidates=candidates,
   238→        )
   239→
   240→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   241→
   242→        model_name = getattr(model, "model_name", "") or getattr(model, "model", "")
   243→        messages = [
   244→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   245→            HumanMessage(content=evaluate_prompt),
   246→        ]
   247→
   248→        with llm_span("medgemma_evaluate", str(model_name)) as llm:
   249→            llm.set_request(f"[system] {system_prompt}\n\n[user] {evaluate_prompt}")
   250→            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   251→            raw_text = raw_response.content
   252→            usage: dict[str, int] = {}
   253→            um = getattr(raw_response, "usage_metadata", None)
   254→            if isinstance(um, dict):
   255→                if um.get("input_tokens"):
   256→                    usage["input_tokens"] = um["input_tokens"]
   257→                if um.get("output_tokens"):
   258→                    usage["output_tokens"] = um["output_tokens"]
   259→                if um.get("total_tokens"):
   260→                    usage["total_tokens"] = um["total_tokens"]
   261→            llm.set_response(str(raw_text), usage or None)
   262→
   263→        logger.debug(
   264→            "MedGemma evaluate response for '%s' (first 200 chars): %s",
   265→            entity_text[:30],
   266→            raw_text[:200],
   267→        )
   268→
   269→        decision = await _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text)
   270→
   271→        logger.info(
   272→            "Grounding decision for '%s': code=%s, system=%s, conf=%.2f",
   273→            entity_text[:50],
   274→            decision.selected_code,
   275→            decision.selected_system,
   276→            decision.confidence,
   277→        )
   278→
   279→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   280→            entity_text=entity_text,
   281→            entity_type=entity_type,
   282→            selected_code=decision.selected_code,
   283→            selected_system=decision.selected_system,
   284→            preferred_term=decision.preferred_term,
   285→            confidence=decision.confidence,
   286→            candidates=candidates,
   287→            reasoning=decision.reasoning,
   288→        )
   289→
   290→    except Exception as e:
   291→        logger.error(
   292→            "MedGemma decision failed for entity '%s': %s",
   293→            entity_text[:50],
   294→            e,
   295→            exc_info=True,
   296→        )
   297→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   298→            entity_text=entity_text,
   299→            entity_type=entity_type,
   300→            selected_code=None,
   301→            selected_system=None,
   302→            preferred_term=None,
   303→            confidence=0.0,
   304→            candidates=candidates,
   305→            reasoning=f"MedGemma decision failed: {e}",
   306→        )

   309→async def _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   310→    """Structure raw MedGemma reasoning output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   311→
   312→    Gemini also acts as a collaborating agent here — it can add its own
   313→    reformulation suggestions via the gemini_suggestion field when structuring
   314→    the output.
   315→
   316→    Args:
   317→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma reasoning output (free-form text).
   318→
   319→    Returns:
   320→        AgenticReasoningResult with structured 3-question answers.
   321→    """
   322→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   323→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   324→
   325→    if not google_api_key:
   326→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   327→
   328→    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   329→
   330→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   331→        model=gemini_model_name,
   332→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   333→    )
   334→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(AgenticReasoningResult)
   335→
   336→    prompt = (
   337→        "Extract the structured reasoning from this medical entity analysis. "
   338→        "Determine: (1) should_skip if entity is not a valid medical criterion, "
   339→        "(2) is_derived and derived_term if entity maps to a standard concept, "
   340→        "(3) rephrased_query if a better medical term would improve search. "
   341→        "You may also add a gemini_suggestion with your own reformulation if "
   342→        "you can improve on the analysis.\n\n"
   343→        f"{raw_text}"
   344→    )
   345→
   346→    with llm_span("gemini_structure_reasoning", gemini_model_name) as llm:
   347→        llm.set_request(prompt)
   348→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   349→        llm.set_response(str(result))
   350→
   351→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   352→        return AgenticReasoningResult.model_validate(result)
   353→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   354→
   355→
   356→async def agentic_reasoning_loop(
   357→    entity: dict,
   358→    criterion_context: str,
   359→    router: "TerminologyRouter",
   360→    attempt: int = 1,
   361→) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   362→    """Ask MedGemma 3 reasoning questions to determine retry strategy.
   363→
   364→    Called by ground_node when an entity fails initial grounding (zero
   365→    candidates or confidence < 0.5). Asks MedGemma in a single prompt:
   366→    - Q1: Is this a valid medical criterion (or should it be skipped)?
   367→    - Q2: Is this a derived entity that maps to a standard concept?
   368→    - Q3: Can this entity be rephrased for better terminology search?
   369→
   370→    Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical reasoning, Gemini
   371→    for structured output parsing (and optional reformulation suggestion).
   372→
   373→    Args:
   374→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   375→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   376→        router: TerminologyRouter instance (for get_apis_for_entity context).
   377→        attempt: Current attempt number (1-3) for prompt context.
   378→
   379→    Returns:
   380→        AgenticReasoningResult with should_skip, is_derived, derived_term,
   381→        rephrased_query, gemini_suggestion, and reasoning fields.
   382→        On error, returns default result (no skip, no rephrase) to allow
   383→        the retry loop to continue with the original query.
   384→    """
   385→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   386→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   387→    previous_query = entity.get("_previous_query", entity_text)
   388→
   389→    logger.info(
   390→        "Agentic reasoning (attempt %d) for entity '%s' (type=%s)",
   391→        attempt,
   392→        entity_text[:50],
   393→        entity_type,
   394→    )
   395→
   396→    try:
   397→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   398→
   399→        system_prompt = _render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   400→        reasoning_prompt = _render_template(
   401→            "grounding_reasoning.jinja2",
   402→            entity_text=entity_text,
   403→            entity_type=entity_type,
   404→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   405→            previous_query=previous_query,
   406→            attempt=attempt,
   407→        )
   408→
   409→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   410→
   411→        model_name = getattr(model, "model_name", "") or getattr(model, "model", "")
   412→        messages = [
   413→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   414→            HumanMessage(content=reasoning_prompt),
   415→        ]
   416→
   417→        with llm_span("medgemma_reasoning", str(model_name)) as llm:
   418→            llm.set_request(f"[system] {system_prompt}\n\n[user] {reasoning_prompt}")
   419→            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   420→            raw_text = raw_response.content
   421→            usage_r: dict[str, int] = {}
   422→            um_r = getattr(raw_response, "usage_metadata", None)
   423→            if isinstance(um_r, dict):
   424→                if um_r.get("input_tokens"):
   425→                    usage_r["input_tokens"] = um_r["input_tokens"]
   426→                if um_r.get("output_tokens"):
   427→                    usage_r["output_tokens"] = um_r["output_tokens"]
   428→                if um_r.get("total_tokens"):
   429→                    usage_r["total_tokens"] = um_r["total_tokens"]
   430→            llm.set_response(str(raw_text), usage_r or None)
   431→
   432→        logger.debug(
   433→            "MedGemma reasoning response for '%s' (first 300 chars): %s",
   434→            entity_text[:30],
   435→            raw_text[:300],
   436→        )
   437→
   438→        result = await _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text)
   439→
   440→        logger.info(
   441→            "Agentic reasoning result for '%s': skip=%s, derived=%s, "
   442→            "derived_term=%s, rephrased=%s",
   443→            entity_text[:50],
   444→            result.should_skip,
   445→            result.is_derived,
   446→            result.derived_term,
   447→            result.rephrased_query,
   448→        )
   449→
   450→        return result
   451→
   452→    except Exception as e:
   453→        logger.error(
   454→            "Agentic reasoning loop failed for entity '%s': %s",
   455→            entity_text[:50],
   456→            e,
   457→            exc_info=True,
   458→        )
   459→        # Return default result (no skip, no rephrase) — retry with original query
   460→        return AgenticReasoningResult(
   461→            should_skip=False,
   462→            is_derived=False,
   463→            derived_term=None,
   464→            rephrased_query=None,
   465→            reasoning=f"Reasoning loop failed: {e}",
   466→        )
   467→
```

```
     1→"""Field mapping generation tool for grounded entities.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "Generate suggested field_mappings during grounding (ground node)"
     4→Per CONTEXT.md: "Criteria should be decomposed per AutoCriteria pattern: separate
     5→Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time"
     6→Per user decision: "MedGemma and Gemini collaborate: Gemini uses MedGemma as
     7→medical expert"
     8→
     9→Uses Gemini to generate suggested field mappings for the grounded entity +
    10→criterion text. These are best-effort suggestions — reviewer can edit in UI.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import logging
    16→from typing import Any, Literal
    17→
    18→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator, model_validator
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    21→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (

    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→# Mapping from legacy/LLM relation operators to the frontend's RelationOperator set
    29→_RELATION_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    30→    "has": "contains",
    31→    "is": "=",
    32→    "not": "not_contains",
    33→    "==": "=",
    34→    "range": "within",
    35→}
    36→
    37→
    38→def _normalize_relation(rel: str) -> str:
    39→    """Normalize a relation operator to the frontend's accepted set.
    40→
    41→    Maps legacy operators (has, is, not, ==, range) to the standard set:
    42→    =, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within, not_in_last, contains, not_contains.
    43→    """
    44→    return _RELATION_MAP.get(rel, rel)
    45→
    46→
    47→class FieldMappingValue(BaseModel):
    48→    """Typed value object for field mappings with type discriminator.
    49→
    50→    Supports three value shapes:
    51→    - standard: single value + unit (e.g. HbA1c < 7%)
    52→    - range: min/max + unit (e.g. Age 18-65 years)
    53→    - temporal: duration + unit (e.g. within 6 months)
    54→    """
    55→
    56→    type: Literal["standard", "range", "temporal"] = Field(
    57→        description="Value type discriminator"
    58→    )
    59→    value: str | None = Field(
    60→        default=None, description="Value for standard type (e.g. '7')"
    61→    )
    62→    unit: str | None = Field(
    63→        default=None, description="Unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'months')"
    64→    )
    65→    min: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Minimum value for range type")
    66→    max: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Maximum value for range type")
    67→    duration: str | None = Field(
    68→        default=None, description="Duration value for temporal type"
    69→    )
    70→
    71→    @model_validator(mode="after")
    72→    def truncate_long_strings(self) -> "FieldMappingValue":
    73→        """Guard against LLM repetition loops producing absurdly long values."""
    74→        _max = 200
    75→        for attr in ("value", "unit", "min", "max", "duration"):
    76→            val = getattr(self, attr)
    77→            if isinstance(val, str) and len(val) > _max:
    78→                setattr(self, attr, val[:_max])
    79→        return self
    80→
    81→
    82→# The set of valid relation operators accepted by the frontend
    83→RelationOperator = Literal[
    84→    "=", "!=", ">", ">=", "<", "<=", "within", "not_in_last", "contains", "not_contains"
    85→]
    86→
    87→
    88→class FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):
    89→    """A single AutoCriteria field mapping decomposition.
    90→
    91→    Per the AutoCriteria pattern, each criterion is decomposed into
    92→    separate Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, and Time components.
    93→    """
    94→
    95→    entity: str = Field(description="The medical entity name (e.g. 'HbA1c')")
    96→    relation: RelationOperator = Field(
    97→        description="The logical operator/relation (e.g. '<', '>', '=', 'contains')"
    98→    )
    99→    value: FieldMappingValue = Field(
   100→        description="Typed value object with type discriminator"
   101→    )
   102→    unit: str | None = Field(
   103→        default=None,
   104→        description="Optional unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')",
   105→    )
   106→    value_concept_id: str | None = Field(
   107→        default=None,
   108→        description="OMOP concept ID for categorical values",
   109→    )
   110→    value_concept_system: str | None = Field(
   111→        default=None,
   112→        description="Terminology system for value_concept_id (e.g. 'SNOMED', 'OMOP')",
   113→    )
   114→
   115→    @field_validator("relation", mode="before")
   116→    @classmethod
   117→    def normalize_relation(cls, v: str) -> str:
   118→        """Normalize LLM-generated relation operators before Literal validation."""
   119→        if isinstance(v, str):
   120→            return _normalize_relation(v)
   121→        return v
   122→
   123→
   124→class FieldMappingResponse(BaseModel):
   125→    """Gemini structured output for field mappings."""
   126→
   127→    mappings: list[FieldMappingItem] = Field(
   128→        default_factory=list,

   131→
   132→
   133→async def generate_field_mappings(
   134→    entity: EntityGroundingResult,
   135→    criterion_text: str,
   136→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   137→    """Generate suggested field mappings for a grounded entity.
   138→
   139→    Uses Gemini to decompose the criterion text into AutoCriteria field mappings:
   140→    Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time components for each discrete condition.
   141→
   142→    This is a best-effort suggestion — reviewer can edit in the UI. Errors
   143→    are logged and an empty list returned (not propagated as failures).
   144→
   145→    Args:
   146→        entity: Grounded EntityGroundingResult with code and preferred term.
   147→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   148→
   149→    Returns:
   150→        List of field mapping dicts with keys: entity, relation, value,
   151→        entity_code, entity_system, omop_concept_id, entity_type.
   152→        Empty list if generation fails.
   153→    """
   154→    if not criterion_text:
   155→        return []
   156→
   157→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)
   158→    if structured_llm is None:
   159→        return []
   160→
   161→    try:
   162→        # Build a context-rich prompt for field mapping generation
   163→        grounded_term = entity.preferred_term or entity.entity_text
   164→        code_context = ""
   165→        if entity.selected_code and entity.selected_system:
   166→            system = entity.selected_system.upper()
   167→            code_context = f"(grounded to {system} code: {entity.selected_code})"
   168→
   169→        prompt = (
   170→            "<role>Clinical trial protocol analyst. Decompose criterion"
   171→            " into Entity-Relation-Value-Unit field mappings.</role>\n\n"
   172→            "<entity_context>\n"
   173→            f"Medical entity: {grounded_term} {code_context}\n"
   174→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n"
   175→            "</entity_context>\n\n"
   176→            "<rules>\n"
   177→            "- Extract each discrete measurement, threshold, or condition"
   178→            " as a separate mapping\n"
   179→            "- relation: one of =, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within,"
   180→            " not_in_last, contains, not_contains\n"
   181→            "- Boolean normalization: presence → relation='=', value='True';"
   182→            " absence → relation='!=', value='True'."
   183→            " Never use 'present'/'absent'/'confirmed' as values.\n"
   184→            "- value types: standard {value, unit},"
   185→            " range {min, max, unit}, temporal {duration, unit}\n"
   186→            "- Temporal duration MUST be a plain number."
   187→            ' "within 5 days" → duration="5", unit="days"\n'
   188→            "- Default: if no measurement, use relation='=',"
   189→            ' value={"type":"standard","value":"True","unit":null}\n'
   190→            "- Relative value resolution: when the criterion expresses"
   191→            " a threshold relative to a reference range or normal limit,"
   192→            " you MUST resolve it to an absolute numeric value with a"
   193→            " standard unit. Use your medical knowledge of the entity's"
   194→            " reference range, compute the absolute threshold, and return"
   195→            " only the computed number in the value field and the standard"
   196→            " lab unit in the unit field. Never return the relative"
   197→            " expression as the value.\n"
   198→            "</rules>\n\n"
   199→            "<anti_patterns>\n"
   200→            "BAD: value='present' → use value='True'\n"
   201→            "BAD: duration='5-days-ago' → use duration='5', unit='days'\n"
   202→            "BAD: value='twice the normal limit' → resolve to absolute\n"
   203→            "</anti_patterns>"
   204→        )
   205→
   206→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   207→
   208→        with llm_span("gemini_field_mapping") as llm:
   209→            llm.set_request(prompt)
   210→            result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   211→            llm.set_response(str(result))
   212→
   213→        response = parse_structured_output(result, FieldMappingResponse)
   214→
   215→        mappings = [
   216→            {
   217→                "entity": m.entity,
   218→                "relation": m.relation,
   219→                "value": m.value.model_dump(exclude_none=True),
   220→                "entity_code": entity.selected_code,
   221→                "entity_system": entity.selected_system,
   222→                "omop_concept_id": entity.omop_concept_id,
   223→                "entity_type": entity.entity_type,
   224→                "value_concept_id": m.value_concept_id,
   225→                "value_concept_system": m.value_concept_system,
   226→            }
   227→            for m in response.mappings
   228→        ]
   229→
   230→        logger.info(
   231→            "Generated %d field mapping(s) for entity '%s'",
   232→            len(mappings),
   233→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   234→        )
   235→        return mappings
   236→
   237→    except Exception as e:
   238→        logger.warning(
   239→            "Field mapping generation failed for entity '%s': %s",
   240→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   241→            e,
   242→            exc_info=True,
   243→        )
   244→        # Best-effort: return empty list on failure
   245→        return []
   246→
```

```
     1→"""Ground node: route entities via TerminologyRouter and select with MedGemma.
     2→
     3→This node is the fourth in the protocol processing pipeline:
     4→ingest→extract→parse→ground→persist
     5→It implements the delegation pattern: thin orchestration that calls tools.
     6→
     7→Per user decisions:
     8→- Error accumulation: entity failures are logged and accumulated, not fatal
     9→- Audit trail: all grounding decisions logged to AuditLog
    10→- TerminologyRouter handles entity-type-aware API routing
    11→- MedGemma selects the best match from candidates
    12→- Field mappings generated per entity for AutoCriteria decomposition
    13→- Consent entities skipped explicitly (not groundable to medical codes)
    14→- Demographics routed normally via umls/snomed (age/gender agentic reasoning)
    15→- Agentic retry loop: max 3 attempts, expert_review routing on exhaustion
    16→
    17→Architecture note: Graph nodes ARE allowed to import from api-service for DB access.
    18→"""
    19→
    20→from __future__ import annotations
    21→
    22→import asyncio
    23→import json
    24→import logging
    25→import os
    26→import re
    27→import time
    28→from typing import Any
    29→
    30→from api_service.storage import engine
    31→from rapidfuzz import fuzz
    32→from shared.models import AuditLog
    33→from sqlmodel import Session
    34→
    35→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    36→from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
    37→from protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper import generate_field_mappings
    38→from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import (
    39→    agentic_reasoning_loop,
    40→    medgemma_decide,
    41→)
    42→from protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper import (
    43→    OmopLookupResult,
    44→    _get_omop_engine,
    45→    lookup_omop_concept,
    46→)
    47→from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import (
    48→    TerminologyRouter,
    49→    _is_likely_acronym,
    50→)
    51→from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import normalize_value
    52→
    53→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    54→
    55→# Singleton router instance (loads YAML config once)
    56→_router: TerminologyRouter | None = None
    57→
    58→
    59→def _get_router() -> TerminologyRouter:
    60→    """Get or create the singleton TerminologyRouter."""
    61→    global _router  # noqa: PLW0603
    62→    if _router is None:
    63→        _router = TerminologyRouter()
    64→    return _router
    65→
    66→
    67→def _reconcile_dual_grounding(
    68→    result: EntityGroundingResult,
    69→    omop_result: OmopLookupResult,
    70→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
    71→    """Reconcile TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper results.
    72→
    73→    Sets omop_concept_id and reconciliation_status on the result:
    74→    - Both succeed → "agree" (high confidence) or "disagreement"
    75→    - Only TerminologyRouter → "omop_missing"
    76→    - Only OMOP → "tooluniverse_missing"
    77→    - Neither → no change (existing expert_review behavior)
    78→
    79→    Agreement is checked by comparing the OMOP concept_name against
    80→    the ToolUniverse preferred_term (fuzzy match).
    81→
    82→    Args:
    83→        result: EntityGroundingResult from TerminologyRouter path.
    84→        omop_result: OmopLookupResult from OMOP mapper path.
    85→
    86→    Returns:
    87→        The same EntityGroundingResult, mutated with OMOP fields.
    88→    """
    89→    tu_ok = result.selected_code is not None
    90→    omop_ok = omop_result.omop_concept_id is not None
    91→
    92→    if tu_ok and omop_ok:
    93→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
    94→        # Fuzzy agreement check using token_set_ratio (order-insensitive)
    95→        tu_term = (result.preferred_term or "").lower()
    96→        omop_term = (omop_result.omop_concept_name or "").lower()
    97→        if tu_term and omop_term and fuzz.token_set_ratio(tu_term, omop_term) >= 80:
    98→            result.reconciliation_status = "agree"
    99→        else:
   100→            result.reconciliation_status = "disagreement"
   101→        logger.info(
   102→            "Dual grounding for '%s': TU=%s, OMOP=%s, status=%s",
   103→            result.entity_text[:50],
   104→            result.selected_code,
   105→            omop_result.omop_concept_id,
   106→            result.reconciliation_status,
   107→        )
   108→    elif tu_ok and not omop_ok:
   109→        result.reconciliation_status = "omop_missing"
   110→    elif not tu_ok and omop_ok:
   111→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
   112→        result.reconciliation_status = "tooluniverse_missing"
   113→    # else: neither — leave as-is (expert_review path)
   114→
   115→    return result
   116→
   117→
   118→def _log_grounding_audit(
   119→    session: Session,
   120→    protocol_id: str,
   121→    criterion_id: str,
   122→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   123→    candidates: list[Any],
   124→    result: Any,
   125→) -> None:
   126→    """Create an AuditLog entry for a grounding decision.
   127→
   128→    Records full details: entity, all candidates considered, selected code,
   129→    reasoning, and confidence. Provides the audit trail per user decision:
   130→    "All agent exchanges (MedGemma decisions, API calls, routing) are logged."
   131→
   132→    Args:
   133→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   134→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
   135→        criterion_id: Criterion ID this entity belongs to.
   136→        entity: Entity dict from entities_json.
   137→        candidates: GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   138→        result: EntityGroundingResult from medgemma_decide.
   139→    """
   140→    audit = AuditLog(
   141→        event_type="entity_grounded",
   142→        target_type="criteria",
   143→        target_id=criterion_id,
   144→        details={
   145→            "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   146→            "entity_text": result.entity_text,
   147→            "entity_type": result.entity_type,
   148→            "candidate_count": len(candidates),
   149→            "candidates": [
   150→                {
   151→                    "source_api": c.source_api,
   152→                    "code": c.code,
   153→                    "preferred_term": c.preferred_term,
   154→                    "score": c.score,
   155→                }
   156→                for c in candidates
   157→            ],
   158→            "selected_code": result.selected_code,
   159→            "selected_system": result.selected_system,
   160→            "preferred_term": result.preferred_term,
   161→            "confidence": result.confidence,
   162→            "reasoning": result.reasoning,
   163→            "omop_concept_id": result.omop_concept_id,
   164→            "reconciliation_status": result.reconciliation_status,
   165→        },
   166→    )
   167→    session.add(audit)
   168→
   169→
   170→def _flush_audit_logs(
   171→    protocol_id: str,
   172→    pending_audits: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], list[Any], Any]],
   173→) -> None:
   174→    """Batch-write audit log entries in a single session.
   175→
   176→    Args:
   177→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
   178→        pending_audits: List of (criterion_id, entity, candidates, result) tuples.
   179→    """
   180→    if not pending_audits:
   181→        return
   182→    try:
   183→        with Session(engine) as session:
   184→            for criterion_id, entity, candidates, result in pending_audits:
   185→                _log_grounding_audit(
   186→                    session,
   187→                    protocol_id,
   188→                    criterion_id,
   189→                    entity,
   190→                    candidates,
   191→                    result,
   192→                )
   193→            session.commit()
   194→    except Exception as audit_error:
   195→        logger.warning(
   196→            "Failed to batch-write %d AuditLog entries: %s",
   197→            len(pending_audits),
   198→            audit_error,
   199→        )
   200→
   201→
   202→async def _ground_entity_with_retry(
   203→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   204→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   205→    criterion_text: str,
   206→) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult, int]:
   207→    """Ground a single entity with agentic retry loop (max 3 attempts).
   208→
   209→    Implements the MedGemma agentic reasoning loop:
   210→    1. Initial grounding via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma
   211→    2. If low confidence, ask MedGemma 3 questions (valid? derived? rephrase?)
   212→    3. Retry with reformulated query (up to 2 more attempts, 3 total)
   213→    4. After 3 failed attempts, mark for expert_review
   214→
   215→    Args:
   216→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   217→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   218→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   219→
   220→    Returns:
   221→        Tuple of (EntityGroundingResult, attempt_count). May have expert_review
   222→        marker in reasoning.
   223→    """
   224→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   225→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   226→
   227→    # Step 1: Route entity through TerminologyRouter to get candidates
   228→    candidates = await router.route_entity(entity_text, entity_type)
   229→
   230→    # Log when TerminologyRouter returns empty
   231→    apis = router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   232→    if not candidates:
   233→        if not apis:
   234→            logger.info(
   235→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) skipped by TerminologyRouter"
   236→                " — no APIs configured for this type",
   237→                entity_text[:50],
   238→                entity_type,
   239→            )
   240→            # No APIs configured → route directly to expert_review
   241→            return (
   242→                EntityGroundingResult(
   243→                    entity_text=entity_text,
   244→                    entity_type=entity_type,
   245→                    selected_code=None,
   246→                    selected_system=None,
   247→                    preferred_term=None,
   248→                    confidence=0.0,
   249→                    candidates=[],
   250→                    reasoning=(
   251→                        "[expert_review] No terminology APIs configured "
   252→                        f"for entity type '{entity_type}'. "
   253→                        "Routed to expert review."
   254→                    ),
   255→                ),
   256→                1,
   257→            )
   258→        else:
   259→            logger.info(
   260→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) returned zero candidates from all APIs",
   261→                entity_text[:50],
   262→                entity_type,
   263→            )
   264→
   265→    # Step 2: MedGemma selects best match from candidates
   266→    result = await medgemma_decide(entity, candidates, criterion_text)
   267→
   268→    # Step 3: Agentic retry loop — max 3 attempts total
   269→    attempt = 1
   270→    current_query = entity_text
   271→    while (
   272→        result.selected_code is None
   273→        and result.confidence < 0.5
   274→        and attempt < 3
   275→        and router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   276→    ):
   277→        attempt += 1
   278→        reasoning_result = await agentic_reasoning_loop(
   279→            entity={**entity, "_previous_query": current_query},
   280→            criterion_context=criterion_text,
   281→            router=router,
   282→            attempt=attempt,
   283→        )
   284→
   285→        if reasoning_result.should_skip:
   286→            logger.info(
   287→                "Entity '%s' marked non-medical by agentic reasoning"
   288→                " (attempt %d) — skipping",
   289→                entity_text[:50],
   290→                attempt,
   291→            )
   292→            return (
   293→                EntityGroundingResult(
   294→                    entity_text=entity_text,
   295→                    entity_type=entity_type,
   296→                    selected_code=None,
   297→                    selected_system=None,
   298→                    preferred_term=None,
   299→                    confidence=0.0,
   300→                    candidates=[],
   301→                    reasoning=(
   302→                        f"Skipped by agentic reasoning (attempt {attempt}): "
   303→                        f"{reasoning_result.reasoning}"
   304→                    ),
   305→                ),
   306→                attempt,
   307→            )
   308→
   309→        new_query = (
   310→            reasoning_result.rephrased_query
   311→            or reasoning_result.derived_term
   312→            or reasoning_result.gemini_suggestion
   313→            or current_query
   314→        )
   315→        current_query = new_query
   316→
   317→        logger.info(
   318→            "Agentic retry (attempt %d) for '%s' using query '%s'",
   319→            attempt,
   320→            entity_text[:50],
   321→            new_query[:50],
   322→        )
   323→
   324→        new_candidates = await router.route_entity(new_query, entity_type)
   325→        if new_candidates:
   326→            retry_entity = {**entity, "text": new_query}
   327→            result = await medgemma_decide(retry_entity, new_candidates, criterion_text)
   328→            if result.selected_code and result.confidence >= 0.5:
   329→                # Success — preserve original entity_text
   330→                return (
   331→                    EntityGroundingResult(
   332→                        entity_text=entity_text,
   333→                        entity_type=result.entity_type,
   334→                        selected_code=result.selected_code,
   335→                        selected_system=result.selected_system,
   336→                        preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   337→                        confidence=result.confidence,
   338→                        candidates=result.candidates,
   339→                        reasoning=(
   340→                            f"[Attempt {attempt}, query='{new_query}'] "
   341→                            f"{result.reasoning}"
   342→                        ),
   343→                        field_mappings=result.field_mappings,
   344→                    ),
   345→                    attempt,
   346→                )
   347→
   348→    # Route to expert_review if all attempts exhausted without success
   349→    if result.selected_code is None and attempt >= 2:
   350→        logger.warning(
   351→            "Entity '%s' (type=%s) routed to expert_review after"
   352→            " %d failed grounding attempts",
   353→            entity_text[:50],
   354→            entity_type,
   355→            attempt,
   356→        )
   357→        return (
   358→            EntityGroundingResult(
   359→                entity_text=result.entity_text,
   360→                entity_type=result.entity_type,
   361→                selected_code=result.selected_code,
   362→                selected_system=result.selected_system,
   363→                preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   364→                confidence=result.confidence,
   365→                candidates=result.candidates,
   366→                reasoning=(
   367→                    f"[expert_review] Routed to expert review after "
   368→                    f"{attempt} failed grounding attempts. "
   369→                    f"Last reasoning: {result.reasoning}"
   370→                ),
   371→            ),
   372→            attempt,
   373→        )
   374→
   375→    return (result, attempt)
   376→
   377→
   378→def _ground_categorical_values(  # noqa: C901
   379→    mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   380→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   381→    """Ground categorical values in field mappings using unit_normalizer.
   382→
   383→    For standard-type mappings with non-numeric values (e.g. "Severe",
   384→    "positive", "NYHA Class III"), attempt to resolve a value_concept_id
   385→    using the unit_normalizer's value lookup.
   386→
   387→    This is a best-effort post-processing step — failures don't block
   388→    the grounding result.
   389→
   390→    Args:
   391→        mappings: List of field mapping dicts from generate_field_mappings.
   392→
   393→    Returns:
   394→        The same list with value_concept_id populated where possible.
   395→    """
   396→    numeric_re = re.compile(r"^[\d.,\-]+$")
   397→
   398→    # Safety-net: normalize common LLM-generated categorical values
   399→    # to canonical boolean form for expression-tree consistency.
   400→    _value_norm = {
   401→        "present": "True",
   402→        "confirmed": "True",
   403→        "positive": "True",
   404→        "yes": "True",
   405→        "absent": "False",
   406→        "negative": "False",
   407→        "not present": "False",
   408→        "no": "False",
   409→    }
   410→
   411→    for mapping in mappings:
   412→        try:
   413→            value_obj = mapping.get("value", {})
   414→            if not isinstance(value_obj, dict):
   415→                continue
   416→
   417→            # Only process standard-type values
   418→            if value_obj.get("type") != "standard":
   419→                continue
   420→
   421→            val_text = value_obj.get("value")
   422→            if not val_text or not isinstance(val_text, str):
   423→                continue
   424→
   425→            # Skip numeric values — they don't need concept grounding
   426→            if numeric_re.match(val_text.strip()):
   427→                continue
   428→
   429→            # Normalize "present"/"confirmed"/"absent" → "True"/"False"
   430→            canonical = _value_norm.get(val_text.strip().lower())
   431→            if canonical is not None:
   432→                value_obj["value"] = canonical
   433→                val_text = canonical
   434→                logger.debug(
   435→                    "Normalized categorical value '%s' → '%s'",
   436→                    val_text,
   437→                    canonical,
   438→                )
   439→
   440→            # If value was normalized to boolean, also normalize relation
   441→            if canonical is not None:
   442→                rel = mapping.get("relation", "")
   443→                if rel in ("contains", "has", "is"):
   444→                    mapping["relation"] = "="
   445→                elif rel in ("not_contains", "not"):
   446→                    mapping["relation"] = "!="
   447→
   448→            # Already grounded
   449→            if mapping.get("value_concept_id"):
   450→                continue
   451→
   452→            # Try unit_normalizer's categorical value lookup (fast path)
   453→            normalized_text, concept_id = normalize_value(val_text)
   454→            if concept_id is not None:
   455→                mapping["value_concept_id"] = str(concept_id)
   456→                mapping["value_concept_system"] = "OMOP"
   457→                logger.debug(
   458→                    "Grounded categorical value '%s' → concept_id=%s",
   459→                    val_text,
   460→                    concept_id,
   461→                )
   462→        except Exception as e:
   463→            logger.debug("Categorical value grounding failed for mapping: %s", e)
   464→
   465→    return mappings
   466→
   467→
   468→async def _ground_entity_parallel(
   469→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   470→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   471→    criterion_text: str,
   472→    entity_num: int,
   473→    total: int,
   474→    semaphore: asyncio.Semaphore,
   475→) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult | None, str | None, float, int]:
   476→    """Ground a single entity with concurrency control and timing.
   477→
   478→    Acquires the semaphore before grounding to cap concurrent API calls.
   479→    Returns a (result, error) tuple for error isolation -- one entity failure
   480→    does not crash others in the asyncio.gather batch.
   481→
   482→    Args:
   483→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   484→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   485→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   486→        entity_num: 1-based index for logging.
   487→        total: Total entity count for logging.
   488→        semaphore: Concurrency limiter (Semaphore(4)).
   489→
   490→    Returns:
   491→        (EntityGroundingResult, None, elapsed_ms, retry_attempts) on success,
   492→        or (None, error_message, elapsed_ms, 0) on failure.
   493→    """
   494→    async with semaphore:
   495→        entity_text = entity.get("text", "")[:50]
   496→        entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   497→        start = time.monotonic()
   498→        logger.info(
   499→            "Grounding entity %d/%d: '%s' (type=%s) — start",
   500→            entity_num,
   501→            total,
   502→            entity_text,
   503→            entity_type,
   504→        )
   505→        try:
   506→            # Dual grounding: run TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper
   507→            # in parallel per entity (Phase 1a)
   508→            full_entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   509→            acronym = _is_likely_acronym(full_entity_text)
   510→            tu_task = _ground_entity_with_retry(entity, router, criterion_text)
   511→            omop_task = lookup_omop_concept(
   512→                full_entity_text, entity_type, is_acronym=acronym
   513→            )
   514→            (result, retry_attempts), omop_result = await asyncio.gather(
   515→                tu_task, omop_task
   516→            )
   517→
   518→            # Reconcile dual grounding results
   519→            result = _reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)
   520→
   521→            # Thread criterion_id from parse into result
   522→            criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id")
   523→            if criterion_id:
   524→                result.criterion_id = criterion_id
   525→
   526→            # Generate field mappings (same semaphore slot)
   527→            field_mappings = await generate_field_mappings(result, criterion_text)
   528→
   529→            # Post-process: ground categorical values in field mappings
   530→            if field_mappings:
   531→                field_mappings = _ground_categorical_values(field_mappings)
   532→
   533→            result.field_mappings = field_mappings if field_mappings else None
   534→
   535→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   536→            elapsed_ms = elapsed * 1000
   537→            logger.info(
   538→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' grounded in %.1fs: code=%s, omop=%s, conf=%.2f",
   539→                entity_num,
   540→                total,
   541→                entity_text,
   542→                elapsed,
   543→                result.selected_code or "N/A",
   544→                result.omop_concept_id or "N/A",
   545→                result.confidence,
   546→            )
   547→            return (result, None, elapsed_ms, retry_attempts)
   548→        except Exception as e:
   549→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   550→            elapsed_ms = elapsed * 1000
   551→            logger.error(
   552→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' failed in %.1fs: %s",
   553→                entity_num,
   554→                total,
   555→                entity_text,
   556→                elapsed,
   557→                e,
   558→            )
   559→            return (None, str(e), elapsed_ms, 0)
   560→
   561→
   562→async def ground_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:  # noqa: C901
   563→    """Ground entities via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma.
   564→
   565→    Parses entities_json from state, routes each entity through TerminologyRouter
   566→    to get terminology candidates, then passes candidates to medgemma_decide for
   567→    best-match selection. Generates field mappings for each successfully grounded
   568→    entity.
   569→
   570→    Error accumulation: individual entity failures are logged and collected in
   571→    errors list. Processing continues with remaining entities. Only a total
   572→    failure to parse entities_json triggers a fatal error.
   573→
   574→    Args:
   575→        state: Current pipeline state with entities_json and protocol_id.
   576→
   577→    Returns:
   578→        Dict with grounded_entities_json and accumulated errors list,
   579→        or fatal error dict if entities_json cannot be parsed.
   580→    """
   581→    from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span
   582→
   583→    if state.get("error"):
   584→        return {}
   585→
   586→    protocol_id = state.get("protocol_id", "")
   587→    with pipeline_span(
   588→        "ground_node", span_type="TOOL", protocol_id=protocol_id
   589→    ) as span:
   590→        span.set_inputs({"protocol_id": state.get("protocol_id", "")})
   591→
   592→        try:
   593→            entities_json = state.get("entities_json")
   594→            if not entities_json:
   595→                span.set_outputs({"error": "No entities_json in state"})
   596→                return {
   597→                    "error": "No entities_json in state — parse node may have failed",
   598→                }
   599→
   600→            entity_items: list[dict[str, Any]] = json.loads(entities_json)
   601→            protocol_id = state["protocol_id"]
   602→
   603→            span.set_inputs(
   604→                {
   605→                    "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   606→                    "entity_count": len(entity_items),
   607→                }
   608→            )
   609→
   610→            logger.info(
   611→                "Starting grounding for protocol %s: %d entities",
   612→                protocol_id,
   613→                len(entity_items),
   614→            )
   615→
   616→            # Fail fast: verify OMOP vocabulary is reachable before
   617→            # starting entity grounding. No silent fallback.
   618→            _get_omop_engine()
   619→
   620→            router = _get_router()
   621→            accumulated_errors: list[str] = list(state.get("errors") or [])
   622→            grounding_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   623→
   624→            # Pre-process: handle consent entities synchronously (skip grounding)
   625→            entities_to_ground: list[tuple[int, dict[str, Any]]] = []
   626→            for idx, entity in enumerate(entity_items, 1):
   627→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   628→                entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   629→
   630→                if entity_type == "Consent":
   631→                    logger.info(
   632→                        "Entity '%s' (type=Consent) skipped — consent/participation"
   633→                        " criteria are not groundable to terminology codes",
   634→                        entity_text[:50],
   635→                    )
   636→                    consent_result = EntityGroundingResult(
   637→                        entity_text=entity_text,
   638→                        entity_type=entity_type,
   639→                        selected_code=None,
   640→                        selected_system=None,
   641→                        preferred_term=None,
   642→                        confidence=0.0,
   643→                        candidates=[],
   644→                        reasoning=(
   645→                            "Consent/participation criterion — not groundable"
   646→                            " to medical terminology codes"
   647→                        ),
   648→                        criterion_id=entity.get("criterion_id"),
   649→                    )
   650→                    grounding_results.append(consent_result.model_dump())
   651→                else:
   652→                    entities_to_ground.append((idx, entity))
   653→
   654→            # Dev/debug: truncate entity list for faster runs and fewer API calls.
   655→            # DEV_MAX_ENTITIES takes precedence over PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES when set.
   656→            _max_entities = int(
   657→                os.getenv("DEV_MAX_ENTITIES")
   658→                or os.getenv("PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES")
   659→                or "0"
   660→            )
   661→            if _max_entities > 0:
   662→                entities_to_ground = entities_to_ground[:_max_entities]
   663→                logger.info(
   664→                    "Max entities limit=%d: truncated to %d entities",
   665→                    _max_entities,
   666→                    len(entities_to_ground),
   667→                )
   668→
   669→            # Pre-flight warmup: absorb MedGemma cold-start latency before
   670→            # the parallel batch. Non-fatal — if warmup fails, entities proceed.
   671→            try:
   672→                from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage as HumanMessageType
   673→
   674→                from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import (
   675→                    _get_medgemma_model,
   676→                )
   677→
   678→                warmup_model = _get_medgemma_model()
   679→                await warmup_model.ainvoke([HumanMessageType(content="ready")])
   680→                logger.info("MedGemma warmup succeeded")
   681→            except Exception as warmup_err:
   682→                logger.info("MedGemma warmup skipped: %s", warmup_err)
   683→
   684→            # Parallel grounding with asyncio.gather + semaphore
   685→            # Semaphore created here (not module-level) to bind to current event loop
   686→            semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)
   687→            num_to_ground = len(entities_to_ground)
   688→
   689→            tasks = [
   690→                _ground_entity_parallel(
   691→                    entity,
   692→                    router,
   693→                    entity.get("criterion_text") or entity.get("text", ""),
   694→                    pos,
   695→                    num_to_ground,
   696→                    semaphore,
   697→                )
   698→                for pos, (idx, entity) in enumerate(entities_to_ground, 1)
   699→            ]
   700→
   701→            outcomes = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
   702→
   703→            # Process outcomes and collect audit entries for batch write
   704→            pending_audits: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], list[Any], Any]] = []
   705→            entity_times: list[float] = []
   706→            total_retry_count = 0
   707→            for (idx, entity), (result, error, elapsed_ms, retries) in zip(
   708→                entities_to_ground, outcomes
   709→            ):
   710→                entity_times.append(elapsed_ms)
   711→                total_retry_count += max(0, retries - 1)  # retries beyond first attempt
   712→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   713→                criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   714→
   715→                if result is not None:
   716→                    pending_audits.append(
   717→                        (criterion_id, entity, result.candidates, result)
   718→                    )
   719→                    grounding_results.append(result.model_dump())
   720→                else:
   721→                    # Entity failed — create expert_review fallback
   722→                    cid_short = criterion_id[:12] if criterion_id else "unknown"
   723→                    error_msg = (
   724→                        f"Entity grounding failed for '{entity_text[:50]}'"
   725→                        f" (criterion={cid_short}): {error}"
   726→                    )
   727→                    logger.error(error_msg)
   728→                    accumulated_errors.append(error_msg)
   729→
   730→            # Batch-write all audit log entries in a single session
   731→            _flush_audit_logs(protocol_id, pending_audits)
   732→
   733→            logger.info(
   734→                "Ground node complete for protocol %s: %d grounded, %d errors",
   735→                protocol_id,
   736→                len(grounding_results),
   737→                len(accumulated_errors),
   738→            )
   739→
   740→            span.set_outputs(
   741→                {
   742→                    "grounded_count": len(grounding_results),
   743→                    "error_count": len(accumulated_errors),
   744→                    "avg_entity_ms": (
   745→                        round(sum(entity_times) / len(entity_times))
   746→                        if entity_times
   747→                        else 0
   748→                    ),
   749→                    "max_entity_ms": round(max(entity_times)) if entity_times else 0,
   750→                    "retry_count": total_retry_count,
   751→                }
   752→            )
   753→
   754→            return {
   755→                "grounded_entities_json": json.dumps(grounding_results),
   756→                "errors": accumulated_errors,
   757→            }
   758→
   759→        except Exception as e:
   760→            logger.exception(
   761→                "Ground node fatal error for protocol %s: %s",
   762→                state.get("protocol_id", "unknown"),
   763→                e,
   764→            )
   765→            span.set_outputs({"error": str(e)})
   766→            return {"error": f"Ground node failed: {e}"}
   767→
```

```
     1→You are a medical terminology expert. Your task is to select the best terminology code for a medical entity extracted from a clinical trial eligibility criterion.
     2→
     3→You will be given:
     4→- The entity text and type
     5→- The criterion context it came from
     6→- A list of terminology candidates from medical APIs (UMLS, SNOMED, RxNorm, etc.)
     7→
     8→Your job: Select the single best match or determine no match exists.
     9→
    10→## Confidence Scoring
    11→
    12→- **0.9-1.0**: Exact match — direct preferred term or recognized synonym
    13→- **0.7-0.8**: Close synonym — different wording, same clinical concept
    14→- **0.5-0.6**: Partial match — related but broader/narrower concept
    15→- **0.0**: No match — wrong domain, not medical, or contradicts context
    16→
    17→## Rules
    18→
    19→1. Prefer exact or near-exact concept matches over broad categories
    20→2. For medications, prefer RxNorm over UMLS when both are available
    21→3. For conditions and procedures, UMLS or SNOMED is appropriate
    22→4. If the entity is a number, unit, or procedural language (not a medical concept), set confidence=0.0
    23→5. Always provide reasoning for your selection
    24→6. Output your analysis in plain text — the output will be structured by a downstream system
    25→7. Text overlap is NOT sufficient for a match. The candidate must represent the same CLINICAL MEANING as the entity in the criterion context. Candidates from the wrong clinical domain (e.g., medical devices or supplies when the entity is a clinical procedure or condition) must receive confidence 0.0 regardless of word overlap.
    26→8. Use the criterion context to validate candidate relevance. Candidates that contradict the criterion's demographic, anatomical, or clinical context must receive confidence 0.0.
    27→
```

```
     1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""Run FULL grounding pipeline against test_snippets.json golden entities.
     3→
     4→Exercises the same code path as the production ground node:
     5→  TerminologyRouter → MedGemma selection → OMOP dual grounding →
     6→  Field mapping generation → Unit normalization
     7→
     8→Usage:
     9→    set -a && source .env && set +a && uv run python tests/e2e/run_grounding_snippets.py
    10→"""
    11→
    12→from __future__ import annotations
    13→
    14→import asyncio
    15→import json
    16→import sys
    17→from pathlib import Path
    18→
    19→sys.path.insert(
    20→    0,
    21→    str(
    22→        Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    23→        / "services"
    24→        / "protocol-processor-service"
    25→        / "src"
    26→    ),
    27→)
    28→sys.path.insert(
    29→    0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "services" / "api-service" / "src")
    30→)
    31→sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "libs" / "shared" / "src"))
    32→
    33→from protocol_processor.nodes.ground import _ground_entity_parallel, _get_router
    34→from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import normalize_unit
    35→
    36→SNIPPETS_PATH = Path(__file__).parent / "test_snippets.json"
    37→
    38→
    39→async def main() -> None:
    40→    data = json.loads(SNIPPETS_PATH.read_text())
    41→    grounding_snippets = data["grounding_test_snippets"]
    42→
    43→    router = _get_router()
    44→    semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)
    45→
    46→    total_entities = 0
    47→    exact_code_match = 0
    48→    grounded_ok = 0
    49→    unit_ucum_match = 0
    50→    unit_omop_match = 0
    51→    total_units = 0
    52→    field_mapping_count = 0
    53→    results: list[dict] = []
    54→
    55→    for snippet_idx, snippet in enumerate(grounding_snippets):
    56→        snippet_text = snippet["snippet_text"]
    57→        print(f"\n{'─' * 70}")
    58→        print(f"Snippet {snippet_idx}: {snippet_text[:80]}...")
    59→        print(f"{'─' * 70}")
    60→
    61→        for ent_idx, golden in enumerate(snippet["entities"]):
    62→            total_entities += 1
    63→            entity_name = golden["entity_name"]
    64→            expected_code = golden["code"]
    65→            expected_system = golden["system"]
    66→            expected_unit = golden.get("unit")
    67→            expected_ucum = golden.get("unit_ucum")
    68→            expected_omop_cid = golden.get("unit_omop_concept_id")
    69→            expected_relation = golden.get("relation")
    70→            golden.get("value")
    71→
    72→            # Build entity dict matching what parse_node produces
    73→            entity = {
    74→                "text": entity_name,
    75→                "entity_type": "Condition",  # generic — router handles dispatch
    76→                "criterion_text": snippet_text,
    77→                "criteria_type": "inclusion",
    78→            }
    79→
    80→            # Run the FULL grounding pipeline
    81→            result, error, elapsed_ms, retries = await _ground_entity_parallel(
    82→                entity,
    83→                router,
    84→                snippet_text,
    85→                entity_num=ent_idx + 1,
    86→                total=len(snippet["entities"]),
    87→                semaphore=semaphore,
    88→            )
    89→
    90→            if error:
    91→                print(f"\n  ENTITY: {entity_name}")
    92→                print(f"    ERROR: {error}")
    93→                results.append(
    94→                    {"entity": entity_name, "grounded": False, "code_match": False}
    95→                )
    96→                continue
    97→
    98→            if result is None:
    99→                print(f"\n  ENTITY: {entity_name}")
   100→                print("    ERROR: no result and no error (unexpected)")
   101→                results.append(
   102→                    {"entity": entity_name, "grounded": False, "code_match": False}
   103→                )
   104→                continue
   105→
   106→            grounded_ok += 1
   107→            code_match = result.selected_code == expected_code
   108→            if code_match:
   109→                exact_code_match += 1
   110→
   111→            # Check field mappings (narrow type for mypy)
   112→            field_mappings = result.field_mappings or []
   113→            has_mappings = bool(field_mappings)
   114→            if has_mappings:
   115→                field_mapping_count += 1
   116→
   117→            # Print entity result
   118→            status_icon = "OK" if code_match else "MISMATCH"
   119→            print(f"\n  ENTITY: {entity_name}")
   120→            print(
   121→                f"    Code [{status_icon}]: expected={expected_system}:{expected_code}"
   122→                f"  got={result.selected_system}:{result.selected_code}"
   123→            )
   124→            print(f"    Preferred term: {result.preferred_term}")
   125→            print(
   126→                f"    Confidence: {result.confidence:.2f}  |  OMOP: {result.omop_concept_id}"
   127→                f"  |  Retries: {retries}  |  Time: {elapsed_ms:.0f}ms"
   128→            )
   129→            if result.reasoning:
   130→                # Truncate long reasoning
   131→                reasoning_short = result.reasoning[:120] + (
   132→                    "..." if len(result.reasoning) > 120 else ""
   133→                )
   134→                print(f"    Reasoning: {reasoning_short}")
   135→
   136→            # Field mappings
   137→            if has_mappings:
   138→                print(f"    Field mappings ({len(field_mappings)}):")
   139→                for fm in field_mappings:
   140→                    rel = fm.get("relation", "?")
   141→                    val = fm.get("value", {})
   142→                    val_str = val.get("value", val.get("min", "?"))
   143→                    unit = val.get("unit", "")
   144→                    print(
   145→                        f"      {fm.get('entity', '?')} {rel} {val_str} {unit}".rstrip()
   146→                    )
   147→            else:
   148→                print("    Field mappings: NONE")
   149→
   150→            # Check relation and value if golden has them
   151→            if expected_relation and has_mappings:
   152→                got_relation = (
   153→                    field_mappings[0].get("relation") if field_mappings else None
   154→                )
   155→                rel_match = got_relation == expected_relation
   156→                print(
   157→                    f"    Relation: expected={expected_relation}  got={got_relation}"
   158→                    f"  {'OK' if rel_match else 'MISMATCH'}"
   159→                )
   160→
   161→            # Unit normalization
   162→            if expected_unit is not None:
   163→                total_units += 1
   164→                got_ucum, got_omop = normalize_unit(expected_unit)
   165→                ucum_ok = got_ucum == expected_ucum
   166→                omop_ok = got_omop == expected_omop_cid
   167→                if ucum_ok:
   168→                    unit_ucum_match += 1
   169→                if omop_ok:
   170→                    unit_omop_match += 1
   171→                unit_status = "OK" if (ucum_ok and omop_ok) else "MISMATCH"
   172→                print(
   173→                    f"    Unit [{unit_status}]: '{expected_unit}' -> UCUM={got_ucum} OMOP={got_omop}"
   174→                )
   175→
   176→            results.append(
   177→                {
   178→                    "entity": entity_name,
   179→                    "grounded": True,
   180→                    "code_match": code_match,
   181→                    "has_mappings": has_mappings,
   182→                }
   183→            )
   184→
   185→    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   186→    # Summary
   187→    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   188→    print(f"\n{'═' * 70}")
   189→    print("FULL PIPELINE RESULTS")
   190→    print(f"{'═' * 70}")
   191→    print(f"  Entities:            {total_entities}")
   192→    print(
   193→        f"  Grounded:            {grounded_ok}/{total_entities}"
   194→        f" ({100 * grounded_ok // max(total_entities, 1)}%)"
   195→    )
   196→    print(
   197→        f"  Exact code match:    {exact_code_match}/{total_entities}"
   198→        f" ({100 * exact_code_match // max(total_entities, 1)}%)"
   199→    )
   200→    print(
   201→        f"  With field mappings: {field_mapping_count}/{total_entities}"
   202→        f" ({100 * field_mapping_count // max(total_entities, 1)}%)"
   203→    )
   204→    print(
   205→        f"  Unit UCUM match:     {unit_ucum_match}/{total_units}"
   206→        f" ({100 * unit_ucum_match // max(total_units, 1)}%)"
   207→    )
   208→    print(
   209→        f"  Unit OMOP match:     {unit_omop_match}/{total_units}"
   210→        f" ({100 * unit_omop_match // max(total_units, 1)}%)"
   211→    )
   212→    print(f"{'═' * 70}")
   213→
   214→    # Failures summary
   215→    failures = [r for r in results if not r.get("code_match")]
   216→    if failures:
   217→        print("\nCode match failures:")
   218→        for f in failures:
   219→            status = "ERROR" if not f["grounded"] else "MISMATCH"
   220→            print(f"  [{status}] {f['entity']}")
   221→
   222→    no_mappings = [
   223→        r for r in results if r.get("grounded") and not r.get("has_mappings")
   224→    ]
   225→    if no_mappings:
   226→        print("\nMissing field mappings:")
   227→        for f in no_mappings:
   228→            print(f"  {f['entity']}")
   229→
   230→
   231→if __name__ == "__main__":
   232→    asyncio.run(main())
   233→
```
