     1→"""Pydantic schemas for terminology grounding results and API candidates.
     2→
     3→These schemas define the machine-readable format for agent-to-agent communication
     4→between TerminologyRouter and MedGemma during the grounding phase.
     5→
     6→Per user decision: "Agent-to-agent communication must be machine-readable
     7→(no superfluous commentary between agents)."
     8→"""
     9→
    10→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    11→
    12→
    13→class GroundingCandidate(BaseModel):
    14→    """A single candidate match from a terminology API.
    15→
    16→    Attributes:
    17→        source_api: Name of the API that produced this candidate.
    18→        code: Terminology code (e.g. UMLS CUI "C0011847", RxNorm "6809").
    19→        preferred_term: Canonical preferred name for the concept.
    20→        semantic_type: UMLS semantic type. None for non-UMLS APIs.
    21→        score: Relevance score from the API (0.0-1.0). Higher is better.
    22→    """
    23→
    24→    source_api: str = Field(
    25→        description="Name of the terminology API that produced this candidate"
    26→    )
    27→    code: str = Field(description="Terminology code (CUI, RxNorm, ICD-10, etc.)")
    28→    preferred_term: str = Field(description="Canonical preferred name for the concept")
    29→    semantic_type: str | None = Field(
    30→        default=None,
    31→        description=(
    32→            "UMLS semantic type (e.g. 'Disease or Syndrome'). None for non-UMLS APIs."
    33→        ),
    34→    )
    35→    score: float = Field(
    36→        ge=0.0,
    37→        le=1.0,
    38→        description="Relevance score from the API (0.0-1.0). Higher is better.",
    39→    )
    40→
    41→
    42→class EntityGroundingResult(BaseModel):
    43→    """Grounding result for a single entity after MedGemma evaluation.
    44→
    45→    Contains the selected code, all candidates considered, and the reasoning
    46→    used to make the selection. Provides full audit trail per user decision:
    47→    "All agent exchanges persisted to database."
    48→
    49→    Attributes:
    50→        entity_text: Original entity text as extracted from the protocol.
    51→        entity_type: Entity type (e.g. "Medication", "Condition").
    52→        selected_code: The chosen terminology code. None if grounding failed.
    53→        selected_system: The coding system for the selected code.
    54→        preferred_term: Canonical name for the selected code.
    55→        confidence: MedGemma's confidence in the selection (0.0-1.0).
    56→        candidates: All candidates returned by terminology APIs.
    57→        reasoning: MedGemma's explanation for selecting this code.
    58→        field_mappings: Suggested AutoCriteria field mappings
    59→            (Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time). None if not yet generated.
    60→    """
    61→
    62→    entity_text: str = Field(description="Original entity text from the protocol")
    63→    entity_type: str = Field(description="Entity type (e.g. 'Medication', 'Condition')")
    64→    selected_code: str | None = Field(
    65→        default=None,
    66→        description="The chosen terminology code. None if grounding failed.",
    67→    )
    68→    selected_system: str | None = Field(
    69→        default=None,
    70→        description="The coding system for the selected code (e.g. 'UMLS').",
    71→    )
    72→    preferred_term: str | None = Field(
    73→        default=None,
    74→        description="Canonical name for the selected code.",
    75→    )
    76→    confidence: float = Field(
    77→        ge=0.0,
    78→        le=1.0,
    79→        description="MedGemma's confidence in the selection (0.0-1.0).",
    80→    )
    81→    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate] = Field(
    82→        default_factory=list,
    83→        description="All candidates returned by terminology APIs.",
    84→    )
    85→    reasoning: str = Field(
    86→        default="",
    87→        description="MedGemma's explanation for selecting this code.",
    88→    )
    89→    field_mappings: list[dict] | None = Field(
    90→        default=None,
    91→        description=(
    92→            "Suggested AutoCriteria field mappings "
    93→            "(Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time)."
    94→        ),
    95→    )
    96→    criterion_id: str | None = Field(
    97→        default=None,
    98→        description=(
    99→            "FK to Criteria record this entity belongs to (threaded from parse)."
   100→        ),
   101→    )
   102→    omop_concept_id: str | None = Field(
   103→        default=None,
   104→        description=(
   105→            "OMOP standard concept_id from Athena vocabulary lookup. "
   106→            "Used for CIRCE export and OMOP CDM joins."
   107→        ),
   108→    )
   109→    reconciliation_status: str | None = Field(
   110→        default=None,
   111→        description=(
   112→            "Dual grounding reconciliation: 'agree', 'disagreement', "
   113→            "'omop_missing', 'tooluniverse_missing'."
   114→        ),
   115→    )
   116→
   117→
   118→class GroundingBatchResult(BaseModel):
   119→    """Batch grounding result for all entities in a criteria batch.
   120→
   121→    Implements the error accumulation pattern — partial failures are preserved
   122→    alongside successes. Per user decision: "Errors accumulate — process all
   123→    entities, collect errors alongside successes (partial results are useful)."
   124→
   125→    Attributes:
   126→        results: Successfully grounded entity results.
   127→        errors: Error messages for entities that failed grounding.
   128→    """
   129→
   130→    results: list[EntityGroundingResult] = Field(
   131→        default_factory=list,
   132→        description="Successfully grounded entity results.",
   133→    )
   134→    errors: list[str] = Field(
   135→        default_factory=list,
   136→        description="Error messages for entities that failed grounding.",
   137→    )
   138→

```
     1→"""Shared Gemini LLM utilities for protocol processor tools.
     2→
     3→Centralizes the repeated pattern of creating a ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
     4→client with structured output and parsing its response. Used by
     5→structure_builder, ordinal_resolver, and field_mapper.
     6→
     7→Falls back to Ollama when GOOGLE_API_KEY is not set but OLLAMA_BASE_URL
     8→is configured.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→import os
    15→from typing import Any, TypeVar
    16→
    17→from pydantic import BaseModel
    18→
    19→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    20→
    21→T = TypeVar("T", bound=BaseModel)
    22→
    23→
    24→def create_structured_llm(
    25→    output_schema: type[T],
    26→) -> Any | None:
    27→    """Create an LLM client with structured output.
    28→
    29→    Tries Gemini first (GOOGLE_API_KEY), falls back to Ollama
    30→    (OLLAMA_BASE_URL) if Gemini is not configured.
    31→
    32→    Args:
    33→        output_schema: Pydantic model class for structured output.
    34→
    35→    Returns:
    36→        A structured LLM instance, or None if no backend is available.
    37→    """
    38→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
    39→    if google_api_key:
    40→        try:
    41→            from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    42→
    43→            gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
    44→            gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
    45→                model=gemini_model_name,
    46→                google_api_key=google_api_key,
    47→            )
    48→            return gemini.with_structured_output(output_schema)
    49→        except Exception as e:
    50→            logger.warning("Failed to create Gemini client: %s", e)
    51→
    52→    # Fallback to Ollama
    53→    ollama_base_url = os.getenv("OLLAMA_BASE_URL")
    54→    if ollama_base_url:
    55→        try:
    56→            from langchain_community.chat_models import ChatOllama
    57→
    58→            ollama_model = os.getenv("OLLAMA_MODEL", "gemma2:9b")
    59→            logger.info(
    60→                "Using Ollama for structured output: %s/%s",
    61→                ollama_base_url,
    62→                ollama_model,
    63→            )
    64→            ollama = ChatOllama(
    65→                base_url=ollama_base_url,
    66→                model=ollama_model,
    67→                format="json",
    68→                temperature=0.1,
    69→            )
    70→            return ollama.with_structured_output(output_schema)
    71→        except Exception as e:
    72→            logger.warning("Failed to create Ollama client: %s", e)
    73→
    74→    logger.warning("No LLM backend available — skipping structured output call")
    75→    return None
    76→
    77→
    78→def parse_structured_output(result: Any, model: type[T]) -> T:
    79→    """Parse a structured LLM output into a Pydantic model.
    80→
    81→    Handles both dict and already-parsed model instances returned
    82→    by LangChain's with_structured_output().
    83→
    84→    Args:
    85→        result: Raw LLM output (dict or model instance).
    86→        model: Pydantic model class to validate against.
    87→
    88→    Returns:
    89→        Validated model instance.
    90→    """
    91→    if isinstance(result, dict):
    92→        return model.model_validate(result)
    93→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
    94→
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/tests/e2e/prompt_variants.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Prompt variant definitions for A/B testing grounding failures.\n\nPure data module — no side effects, no imports beyond stdlib.\n\nEach MedGemma variant has:\n- name: short label\n- grounding_system: extra text to append to grounding_system.jinja2 output (or None)\n- grounding_evaluate: extra text to append to grounding_evaluate.jinja2 output (or None)\n\nEach field mapper variant has:\n- name: short label\n- field_mapper_rules_extra: extra rules to inject before </rules> in prompt (or None)\n\"\"\"\n\n# ── MedGemma variant fragments ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n_SPECIFICITY_GUARD_SYSTEM = (\n    \"\\n9. When the entity text contains no qualifier (e.g., no age, stage, \"\n    \"site, or laterality modifier), prefer the PARENT concept over \"\n    \"subspecific variants. For example, 'Ankylosing Spondylitis' maps to \"\n    \"the general concept (C0038013), not 'Juvenile Ankylosing Spondylitis'.\"\n    \"\\n10. When multiple candidates share near-identical preferred terms \"\n    \"(differing only by version, alternate CUI, or source), prefer the \"\n    \"PRIMARY CUI — the one whose preferred term is the most concise and \"\n    \"canonical match for the entity text.\"\n)\n\n_CONTEXT_EMPHASIS_EVALUATE = (\n    \"\\n\\n## Cross-check\\n\"\n    \"Before making your final selection, verify:\\n\"\n    \"- If the candidate's preferred term adds a qualifier NOT present in \"\n    \"the entity text (e.g., 'Juvenile', 'Ectopic', 'Chronic', 'Bilateral'), \"\n    \"it is OVER-SPECIFIC — prefer the unqualified parent concept.\\n\"\n    \"- If two candidates have near-identical preferred terms, prefer the \"\n    \"one whose CUI is the primary/canonical entry, not an alternate or \"\n    \"version-specific code.\"\n)\n\n# ── Field mapper variant fragments ──────────────────────────────────────────\n\n_OPERATOR_RULES_EXTRA = (\n    \"\\n- Extract comparison operators VERBATIM from the criterion text. \"\n    \"Never invert the operator direction. If the text says \\\">44\\\", \"\n    \"the relation is \\\">\\\", not \\\"<\\\".\"\n    \"\\n- Use \\\"within\\\" ONLY for numeric min/max ranges (e.g., \\\"between \"\n    \"18 and 65\\\"). Do NOT use \\\"within\\\" for discrete categorical lists \"\n    \"like \\\"1, 2, or 3\\\" — use \\\"=\\\" instead.\"\n    \"\\n- Negation patterns: \\\"non-\\\", \\\"not \\\", \\\"no \\\" preceding an entity \"\n    \"indicate ABSENCE. Map to relation='!=' with value='True'. \"\n    \"For example, \\\"non-pregnant\\\" means Pregnancy relation='!=' value='True'.\"\n)\n\n# ── Variant lists ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nMEDGEMMA_VARIANTS: list[dict] = [\n    {\n        \"name\": \"baseline\",\n        \"grounding_system\": None,\n        \"grounding_evaluate\": None,\n    },\n    {\n        \"name\": \"specificity_guard\",\n        \"grounding_system\": _SPECIFICITY_GUARD_SYSTEM,\n        \"grounding_evaluate\": None,\n    },\n    {\n        \"name\": \"context_emphasis\",\n        \"grounding_system\": None,\n        \"grounding_evaluate\": _CONTEXT_EMPHASIS_EVALUATE,\n    },\n    {\n        \"name\": \"combined\",\n        \"grounding_system\": _SPECIFICITY_GUARD_SYSTEM,\n        \"grounding_evaluate\": _CONTEXT_EMPHASIS_EVALUATE,\n    },\n]\n\nFIELD_MAPPER_VARIANTS: list[dict] = [\n    {\n        \"name\": \"baseline\",\n        \"field_mapper_rules_extra\": None,\n    },\n    {\n        \"name\": \"operator_rules\",\n        \"field_mapper_rules_extra\": _OPERATOR_RULES_EXTRA,\n    },\n]\n"
}

for vn in variant_names}\n    for ent in RELATION_MISMATCH_ENTITIES:\n        label = f\"{ent['snippet_idx']}:{ent['entity_idx']}\"\n        name = ent[\"entity_name\"][:24]\n        expected = ent[\"expected_relation\"]\n        row = f\"{name:<26} {expected:<12}\"\n        for vn in variant_names:\n            got = relation_results[vn].get(label, \"?\")\n            match = got == expected\n            if match:\n                totals[vn] += 1\n            marker = \"OK\" if match else (got or \"None\")[:14]\n            row += f\" {marker:<16}\"\n        print(row)\n\n    # Totals\n    total_ents = len(RELATION_MISMATCH_ENTITIES)\n    print(\"-\" * len(header))\n    totals_row = f\"{'Totals:':<26} {'':12}\"\n    for vn in variant_names:\n        totals_row += f\" {totals[vn]}/{total_ents:<14}\"\n    print(totals_row)\n\n\n# ── Main ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\nasync def main() -> None:\n    data = json.loads(SNIPPETS_PATH.read_text())\n    snippets = data[\"grounding_test_snippets\"]\n\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"PROMPT A/B TESTING HARNESS\")\n    print(f\"  Code mismatch entities: {len(CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES)}\")\n    print(f\"  Relation mismatch entities: {len(RELATION_MISMATCH_ENTITIES)}\")\n    print(f\"  MedGemma variants: {len(MEDGEMMA_VARIANTS)}\")\n    print(f\"  Field mapper variants: {len(FIELD_MAPPER_VARIANTS)}\")\n    llm_calls = (\n        len(CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES) * len(MEDGEMMA_VARIANTS)\n        + len(RELATION_MISMATCH_ENTITIES) * len(FIELD_MAPPER_VARIANTS)\n    )\n    print(\n        f\"  Estimated LLM calls: ~{llm_calls} \"\n        f\"(+ {len(CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES)} router lookups)\"\n    )\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n\n    start = time.monotonic()\n\n    # ── Code mismatch tests ──\n    print(\"\\n[1/2] CODE SELECTION TESTS\")\n    code_results = await _run_code_mismatch_tests(snippets)\n\n    # ── Relation mismatch tests ──\n    print(\"\\n[2/2] RELATION EXTRACTION TESTS\")\n    relation_results = await _run_relation_mismatch_tests(snippets)\n\n    elapsed = time.monotonic() - start\n\n    # ── Reports ──\n    _print_code_report(code_results)\n    _print_relation_report(relation_results)\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal time: {elapsed:.1f}s\")\n\n    # ── Save JSON results ──\n    if os.getenv(\"SAVE_RESULTS\", \"1\") == \"1\":\n        RESULTS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        output = {\n            \"code_selection\": code_results,\n            \"relation_extraction\": relation_results,\n            \"entities\": {\n                \"code_mismatch\": CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES,\n                \"relation_mismatch\": [\n                    {\n                        k: v\n                        for k, v in ent.items()\n                        if k\n                        not in (\n                            \"golden_code\",\n                            \"golden_system\",\n                            \"golden_preferred_term\",\n                        )\n                    }\n                    for ent in RELATION_MISMATCH_ENTITIES\n                ],\n            },\n            \"variants\": {\n                \"medgemma\": [v[\"name\"] for v in MEDGEMMA_VARIANTS],\n                \"field_mapper\": [v[\"name\"] for v in FIELD_MAPPER_VARIANTS],\n            },\n        }\n        out_path = RESULTS_DIR / \"latest.json\"\n        out_path.write_text(json.dumps(output, indent=2))\n        print(f\"Results saved to {out_path}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    asyncio.run(main())\n"
}

> AGENT

Both files are new (untracked), no production files modified. Here's a summary:

## Created Files

**`tests/e2e/prompt_variants.py`** — Pure data module with variant definitions:
- 4 MedGemma variants: `baseline`, `specificity_guard` (2 extra system rules), `context_emphasis` (cross-check appended to evaluate), `combined` (both)
- 2 Field mapper variants: `baseline`, `operator_rules` (3 extra rules for verbatim operators, within-only-for-ranges, negation patterns)

**`tests/e2e/run_prompt_variants.py`** — Main test harness:
- **Code mismatch tests**: Caches TerminologyRouter candidates per entity (6 calls), then runs `medgemma_decide` with patched `_render_template` for each of 4 variants (24 LLM calls)
- **Relation mismatch tests**: Creates synthetic `EntityGroundingResult` from golden data, calls `generate_field_mappings` with patched `create_structured_llm` for 2 variants (6 LLM calls)
- **Monkey-patching**: `_make_patched_render()` appends variant text to rendered template output; `_make_field_mapper_llm_wrapper()` wraps the structured LLM's `ainvoke` to inject extra rules before `</rules>`
- **Report output**: Two comparison tables (code selection + relation extraction) with per-entity results and totals
- **JSON export**: Saves results to `tests/e2e/prompt_variant_results/latest.json`

## To Run

```bash
set -a && source .env && set +a && uv run python tests/e2e/run_prompt_variants.py
```

> AGENT

Run completed (19 minutes). Here are the results:

## CODE SELECTION (MedGemma variants)

| Entity | Expected | baseline | specificity | context | combined |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Serum creatinine | C0201975 | C0201976 | C0201976 | C0201976 | C0201976 |
| eGFR | C0858118 | C3811844 | C3811844 | C3811844 | C3811844 |
| Ankylosing Spondylitis | C0038013 | C0409675 | **OK** | **OK** | C0409675 |
| Radiologic examination | C0034693 | C0043299 | C0043299 | C0043299 | C0043299 |
| GBA gene mutation | C4225361 | C3888963 | C3888963 | C3888963 | C3888963 |
| Pregnancy | C0032961 | **OK** | **OK** | **OK** | **OK** |
| **Totals** | | **1/6** | **2/6** | **2/6** | **1/6** |

**Findings:** `specificity_guard` and `context_emphasis` both fixed Ankylosing Spondylitis (parent concept preference worked). Oddly, `combined` did not — possible LLM non-determinism. Pregnancy was already correct at baseline (1/6 not 0/6 as expected). The remaining 4 mismatches (Serum creatinine, eGFR, Radiologic exam, GBA mutation) are stubbornly resistant — they'll need different strategies.

## RELATION EXTRACTION (Field mapper variants)

| Entity | Expected | baseline | operator_rules |
|---|---|---|---|
| Body Mass Index | `>` | `<` | ERROR |
| ASA physical status | `=` | `<=` | ERROR |
| Pregnancy | `!=` | **OK** | ERROR |
| **Totals** | | **1/3** | **0/3** |

**Issue:** The `operator_rules` variant failed with `"RunnableSequence" object has no field "ainvoke"`. The `create_structured_llm` returns a LangChain `RunnableSequence` (from `.with_structured_output()`), not a plain object — so monkey-patching `.ainvoke` as an attribute doesn't work since Pydantic/RunnableSequence rejects arbitrary field assignment.

Let me fix the field mapper patching to wrap the RunnableSequence properly.

> TOOL

The `RunnableSequence` from LangChain doesn't allow attribute assignment. I need to use a wrapper class instead.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_012b9jQZDsXKur1ALcTf4z5X
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/tests/e2e/run_prompt_variants.py",
  "old_string": "def _make_field_mapper_llm_wrapper(extra_rules: str):\n    \"\"\"Create a patched create_structured_llm that injects extra rules.\n\n    Returns a drop-in replacement for create_structured_llm. The returned\n    LLM's ainvoke method modifies the prompt to insert extra rules before\n    the </rules> closing tag.\n    \"\"\"\n    from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (\n        create_structured_llm as _real_create,\n    )\n\n    def patched_create(output_schema):\n        real_llm = _real_create(output_schema)\n        if real_llm is None:\n            return None\n\n        real_ainvoke = real_llm.ainvoke\n\n        async def modified_ainvoke(prompt, *args, **kwargs):\n            modified_prompt = prompt.replace(\n                \"</rules>\", extra_rules + \"\\n</rules>\"\n            )\n            return await real_ainvoke(modified_prompt, *args, **kwargs)\n\n        real_llm.ainvoke = modified_ainvoke\n        return real_llm\n\n    return patched_create",
  "new_string": "class _PromptModifyingLLM:\n    \"\"\"Wrapper around a LangChain RunnableSequence that modifies prompts.\n\n    LangChain's .with_structured_output() returns a RunnableSequence (Pydantic\n    model) that doesn't allow attribute assignment. This wrapper delegates all\n    calls to the real LLM but injects extra rules into the prompt.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, real_llm, extra_rules: str):\n        self._real = real_llm\n        self._extra = extra_rules\n\n    async def ainvoke(self, prompt, *args, **kwargs):\n        modified = prompt.replace(\"</rules>\", self._extra + \"\\n</rules>\")\n        return await self._real.ainvoke(modified, *args, **kwargs)\n\n    def invoke(self, prompt, *args, **kwargs):\n        modified = prompt.replace(\"</rules>\", self._extra + \"\\n</rules>\")\n        return self._real.invoke(modified, *args, **kwargs)\n\n    def __getattr__(self, name):\n        return getattr(self._real, name)\n\n\ndef _make_field_mapper_llm_wrapper(extra_rules: str):\n    \"\"\"Create a patched create_structured_llm that injects extra rules.\n\n    Returns a drop-in replacement for create_structured_llm. The returned\n    wrapper modifies prompts to insert extra rules before </rules>.\n    \"\"\"\n    from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (\n        create_structured_llm as _real_create,\n    )\n\n    def patched_create(output_schema):\n        real_llm = _real_create(output_schema)\n        if real_llm is None:\n            return None\n        return _PromptModifyingLLM(real_llm, extra_rules)\n\n    return patched_create"
}

### CODE SELECTION (MedGemma prompt changes)

| Entity | Expected | baseline | specificity_guard | context_emphasis | combined |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Serum creatinine | C0201975 | C0201976 | C0201976 | C0201976 | C0201976 |
| eGFR | C0858118 | C3811844 | C3811844 | C3811844 | C3811844 |
| Ankylosing Spondylitis | C0038013 | C0409675 | **OK** | **OK** | C0409675 |
| Radiologic examination | C0034693 | C0043299 | C0043299 | C0043299 | C0043299 |
| GBA gene mutation | C4225361 | C3888963 | C3888963 | C3888963 | C3888963 |
| Pregnancy | C0032961 | **OK** | **OK** | **OK** | **OK** |
| **Totals** | | **1/6** | **2/6** | **2/6** | **1/6** |

### RELATION EXTRACTION (Field mapper prompt changes)

| Entity | Expected | baseline | operator_rules |
|---|---|---|---|
| Body Mass Index | `>` | `<` | `<` |
| ASA physical status | `=` | `within` | **OK** |
| Pregnancy | `!=` | **OK** | **OK** |
| **Totals** | | **1/3** | **2/3** |

### Key Takeaways

**Code selection:**
- `specificity_guard` and `context_emphasis` both fix Ankylosing Spondylitis (+1 over baseline) — the parent-concept-preference rule works
- `combined` regressed on AS (LLM non-determinism) — the two prompt additions may interact poorly
- 4 entities remain stubbornly wrong (off-by-one CUI for Serum creatinine, wrong concept family for eGFR/Radiologic exam/GBA) — will need different strategies like candidate filtering or few-shot examples

**Relation extraction:**
- `operator_rules` fixes ASA physical status (`within` -> `=`) — the "within only for numeric ranges" rule worked
- BMI operator inversion (`>` expected, `<` returned) persists in both variants — may need a stronger directive or few-shot example
- Pregnancy negation was already correct at baseline

     1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""A/B testing harness for prompt variants on grounding failures.

   126→# ── Patching helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   127→
   128→
   129→def _make_patched_render(variant: dict):
   130→    """Create a patched _render_template for the given MedGemma variant.
   131→
   132→    The wrapper calls the original _render_template, then appends variant-
   133→    specific text to the rendered output for targeted template names.
   134→    """
   135→
   136→    def patched(template_name: str, **kwargs) -> str:
   137→        rendered = _original_render_template(template_name, **kwargs)
   138→        if (
   139→            template_name == "grounding_system.jinja2"
   140→            and variant.get("grounding_system")
   141→        ):
   142→            rendered += variant["grounding_system"]
   143→        if (
   144→            template_name == "grounding_evaluate.jinja2"
   145→            and variant.get("grounding_evaluate")
   146→        ):
   147→            rendered += variant["grounding_evaluate"]
   148→        return rendered
   149→
   150→    return patched
   151→
   152→
   153→class _PromptModifyingLLM:
   154→    """Wrapper around a LangChain RunnableSequence that modifies prompts.
   155→
   156→    LangChain's .with_structured_output() returns a RunnableSequence (Pydantic
   157→    model) that doesn't allow attribute assignment. This wrapper delegates all
   158→    calls to the real LLM but injects extra rules into the prompt.
   159→    """
   160→
   161→    def __init__(self, real_llm, extra_rules: str):
   162→        self._real = real_llm
   163→        self._extra = extra_rules
   164→
   165→    async def ainvoke(self, prompt, *args, **kwargs):
   166→        modified = prompt.replace("</rules>", self._extra + "\n</rules>")
   167→        return await self._real.ainvoke(modified, *args, **kwargs)
   168→
   169→    def invoke(self, prompt, *args, **kwargs):
   170→        modified = prompt.replace("</rules>", self._extra + "\n</rules>")
   171→        return self._real.invoke(modified, *args, **kwargs)
   172→
   173→    def __getattr__(self, name):
   174→        return getattr(self._real, name)
   175→
   176→
   177→def _make_field_mapper_llm_wrapper(extra_rules: str):
   178→    """Create a patched create_structured_llm that injects extra rules.
   179→
   180→    Returns a drop-in replacement for create_structured_llm. The returned
   181→    wrapper modifies prompts to insert extra rules before </rules>.
   182→    """
   183→    from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
   184→        create_structured_llm as _real_create,
   185→    )
   186→
   187→    def patched_create(output_schema):
   188→        real_llm = _real_create(output_schema)
   189→        if real_llm is None:
   190→            return None
   191→        return _PromptModifyingLLM(real_llm, extra_rules)
   192→
   193→    return patched_create
   194→
   195→
   196→# ── Code mismatch testing ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   197→
   198→
   199→async def _run_code_mismatch_tests(
   200→    snippets: list[dict],
   201→) -> dict[str, dict[str, str | None]]:
   202→    """Run code mismatch entities through each MedGemma variant.
   203→
   204→    Step 1: Cache TerminologyRouter candidates per entity (one API call each).
   205→    Step 2: For each variant, patch _render_template and call medgemma_decide.
   206→
   207→    Returns: {variant_name: {entity_label: selected_code_or_None}}
   208→    """
   209→    router = _get_router()
   210→    results: dict[str, dict[str, str | None]] = {}
   211→
   212→    # Step 1: Cache candidates
   213→    print("\n  Caching TerminologyRouter candidates...")
   214→    cached_candidates: dict[str, list] = {}
   215→    for ent in CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES:
   216→        label = f"{ent['snippet_idx']}:{ent['entity_idx']}"
   217→        entity_name = ent["entity_name"]
   218→        candidates = await router.route_entity(entity_name, "Condition")
   219→        cached_candidates[label] = candidates
   220→        print(f"    {label} '{entity_name}': {len(candidates)} candidates")
   221→
   222→    # Step 2: Run each variant
   223→    for variant in MEDGEMMA_VARIANTS:
   224→        vname = variant["name"]
   225→        print(f"\n  Variant: {vname}")
   226→        results[vname] = {}
   227→
   228→        for ent in CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES:
   229→            label = f"{ent['snippet_idx']}:{ent['entity_idx']}"
   230→            entity_name = ent["entity_name"]
   231→            snippet = snippets[ent["snippet_idx"]]
   232→            criterion_text = snippet["snippet_text"]
   233→            candidates = cached_candidates[label]
   234→
   235→            entity_dict = {
   236→                "text": entity_name,
   237→                "entity_type": "Condition",
   238→                "criterion_text": criterion_text,
   239→            }
   240→
   241→            patched_render = _make_patched_render(variant)
   242→            with patch(
   243→                "protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider._render_template",
   244→                new=patched_render,
   245→            ):
   246→                try:
   247→                    result = await medgemma_decide(
   248→                        entity_dict, candidates, criterion_text
   249→                    )
   250→                    code = result.selected_code
   251→                except Exception as e:
   252→                    print(f"    {label} '{entity_name}': ERROR — {e}")
   253→                    code = None
   254→
   255→            match = "OK" if code == ent["expected_code"] else "MISS"
   256→            print(f"    {label} '{entity_name}': {code} [{match}]")
   257→            results[vname][label] = code
   258→
   259→    return results
   260→
   261→
   262→# ── Relation mismatch testing ──────────────────────────────────────────────
   263→
   264→
   265→async def _run_relation_mismatch_tests(
   266→    snippets: list[dict],
   267→) -> dict[str, dict[str, str | None]]:
   268→    """Run relation mismatch entities through each field mapper variant.
   269→
   270→    Uses synthetic EntityGroundingResult objects built from golden data
   271→    (no TerminologyRouter or MedGemma calls needed).
   272→
   273→    Returns: {variant_name: {entity_label: relation_or_None}}
   274→    """
   275→    results: dict[str, dict[str, str | None]] = {}
   276→
   277→    for variant in FIELD_MAPPER_VARIANTS:
   278→        vname = variant["name"]
   279→        print(f"\n  Variant: {vname}")
   280→        results[vname] = {}
   281→
   282→        for ent in RELATION_MISMATCH_ENTITIES:
   283→            label = f"{ent['snippet_idx']}:{ent['entity_idx']}"
   284→            snippet = snippets[ent["snippet_idx"]]
   285→            criterion_text = snippet["snippet_text"]
   286→
   287→            # Build synthetic EntityGroundingResult from golden data
   288→            entity_result = EntityGroundingResult(
   289→                entity_text=ent["entity_name"],
   290→                entity_type="Condition",
   291→                selected_code=ent["golden_code"],
   292→                selected_system=ent["golden_system"],
   293→                preferred_term=ent["golden_preferred_term"],
   294→                confidence=0.9,
   295→                candidates=[],
   296→                reasoning="A/B test harness — golden code used",
   297→            )
   298→
   299→            extra_rules = variant.get("field_mapper_rules_extra")
   300→            try:
   301→                if extra_rules:
   302→                    wrapper = _make_field_mapper_llm_wrapper(extra_rules)
   303→                    with patch(
   304→                        "protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper.create_structured_llm",
   305→                        new=wrapper,
   306→                    ):
   307→                        mappings = await generate_field_mappings(
   308→                            entity_result, criterion_text
   309→                        )
   310→                else:
   311→                    mappings = await generate_field_mappings(
   312→                        entity_result, criterion_text
   313→                    )
   314→                relation = mappings[0].get("relation") if mappings else None
   315→            except Exception as e:
   316→                print(f"    {label} '{ent['entity_name']}': ERROR — {e}")
   317→                relation = None
   318→
   319→            match = "OK" if relation == ent["expected_relation"] else "MISS"
   320→            print(f"    {label} '{ent['entity_name']}': {relation} [{match}]")
   321→            results[vname][label] = relation
   322→
   323→    return results
   324→

   326→# ── Report printing ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   327→
   328→
   329→def _print_code_report(code_results: dict) -> None:
   330→    """Print the code selection comparison table."""
   331→    print(f"\n{'=' * 80}")
   332→    print("CODE SELECTION VARIANTS (MedGemma prompt changes)")
   333→    print(f"{'=' * 80}")
   334→
   335→    variant_names = list(code_results.keys())
   336→
   337→    # Header
   338→    header = f"{'Entity':<26} {'Expected':<12}"
   339→    for vn in variant_names:
   340→        header += f" {vn:<14}"
   341→    print(header)
   342→    print("-" * len(header))
   343→
   344→    # Rows
   345→    totals = {vn: 0 for vn in variant_names}
   346→    for ent in CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES:
   347→        label = f"{ent['snippet_idx']}:{ent['entity_idx']}"
   348→        name = ent["entity_name"][:24]
   349→        expected = ent["expected_code"]
   350→        row = f"{name:<26} {expected:<12}"
   351→        for vn in variant_names:
   352→            got = code_results[vn].get(label, "?")
   353→            match = got == expected
   354→            if match:
   355→                totals[vn] += 1
   356→            marker = "OK" if match else (got or "None")[:12]
   357→            row += f" {marker:<14}"
   358→        print(row)
   359→
   360→    # Totals
   361→    total_ents = len(CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES)
   362→    print("-" * len(header))
   363→    totals_row = f"{'Totals:':<26} {'':12}"
   364→    for vn in variant_names:
   365→        totals_row += f" {totals[vn]}/{total_ents:<12}"

   412→async def main() -> None:
   413→    run_codes = "--relations-only" not in sys.argv
   414→    run_relations = "--codes-only" not in sys.argv
   415→
   416→    data = json.loads(SNIPPETS_PATH.read_text())

   424→    if run_relations:

   426→        print(f"  Field mapper variants: {len(FIELD_MAPPER_VARIANTS)}")
   427→    print("=" * 80)

   430→
   431→    code_results: dict = {}
   432→    relation_results: dict = {}
   433→
   434→    # ── Code mismatch tests ──
   435→    if run_codes:
   436→        print("\n[1/2] CODE SELECTION TESTS")
   437→        code_results = await _run_code_mismatch_tests(snippets)
   438→
   439→    # ── Relation mismatch tests ──
   440→    if run_relations:
   441→        print("\n[2/2] RELATION EXTRACTION TESTS")
   442→        relation_results = await _run_relation_mismatch_tests(snippets)
   443→
   444→    elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   445→
   446→    # ── Reports ──
   447→    if code_results:
   448→        _print_code_report(code_results)
   449→    if relation_results:
   450→        _print_relation_report(relation_results)
   451→
   452→    print(f"\nTotal time: {elapsed:.1f}s")
   453→
   454→    # ── Save JSON results ──
   455→    if os.getenv("SAVE_RESULTS", "1") == "1":
   456→        RESULTS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   457→        output = {
   458→            "code_selection": code_results,
   459→            "relation_extraction": relation_results,
   460→            "entities": {
   461→                "code_mismatch": CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES,
   462→                "relation_mismatch": [
   463→                    {
   464→                        k: v
   465→                        for k, v in ent.items()
   466→                        if k
   467→                        not in (
   468→                            "golden_code",
   469→                            "golden_system",
   470→                            "golden_preferred_term",
   471→                        )
   472→                    }
   473→                    for ent in RELATION_MISMATCH_ENTITIES
   474→                ],
   475→            },
   476→            "variants": {
   477→                "medgemma": [v["name"] for v in MEDGEMMA_VARIANTS],
   478→                "field_mapper": [v["name"] for v in FIELD_MAPPER_VARIANTS],
   479→            },
   480→        }
   481→        out_path = RESULTS_DIR / "latest.json"
   482→        out_path.write_text(json.dumps(output, indent=2))
   483→        print(f"Results saved to {out_path}")
   484→
   485→
   486→if __name__ == "__main__":
   487→    asyncio.run(main())
   488→
```

> AGENT

Now I have the full picture, and it tells a very different story than the codes suggested. Here's the detailed report:

---

## CODE SELECTION — What MedGemma Actually Picked

**"Baseline"** = the current production prompts with no changes. The other variants add extra rules to steer MedGemma.

| # | Snippet | Entity | Golden Code | Golden Means... | MedGemma Picked | That Means... | Verdict |
|---|---------|--------|------------|-----------------|-----------------|---------------|---------|
| 1 | "Serum creatinine >1.5 times ULN" | Serum creatinine | C0201975 | **Creatinine measurement** (generic) | C0201976 | **Creatinine measurement, serum (procedure)** | Model is arguably MORE correct — "serum" is in the criterion text |
| 2 | "eGFR <45 ml/min" | eGFR | C0858118 | **Plasma creatinine increased** | C3811844 | **Estimated Glomerular Filtration Rate** | **Golden data is WRONG.** C0858118 is about creatinine, not eGFR. Model is correct. |
| 3 | "Diagnosis of active AS with radiologic evidence" | Ankylosing Spondylitis | C0038013 | **Ankylosing spondylitis** | C0409675 | **Juvenile ankylosing spondylitis** | Model over-specifies. `specificity_guard` and `context_emphasis` both fix this. |
| 4 | Same snippet | Radiologic examination | C0034693 | **Rattus norvegicus** (a rat!) | C0043299 | **Diagnostic radiologic examination** | **Golden data is WRONG.** C0034693 is a rat species. Model is correct. |
| 5 | "heterozygous carriers of a GBA mutation" | GBA gene mutation | C4225361 | **Developmental and Epileptic Encephalopathy 29** | C3888963 | **GBA gene mutation** | **Golden data is WRONG.** C4225361 is an encephalopathy. Model is correct. |
| 6 | "non-pregnant females" | Pregnancy | C0032961 | **Pregnancy** | C0032961 | **Pregnancy** | Match in all variants |

### Variant comparison (only row 3 differs):

| Variant | What it does | Ankylosing Spondylitis result |
|---------|-------------|-------------------------------|
| `baseline` | Current production prompts, no changes | C0409675 Juvenile AS (wrong) |
| `specificity_guard` | Adds rules: prefer parent concept when no qualifier in text; prefer primary CUI | C0038013 AS (correct) |
| `context_emphasis` | Adds cross-check: if candidate adds qualifier not in entity text, it's over-specific | C0038013 AS (correct) |
| `combined` | Both above together | C0409675 Juvenile AS (wrong — LLM non-determinism) |

---

## RELATION EXTRACTION — Operator Selection

**"Baseline"** = current field mapper prompt. **"operator_rules"** adds 3 rules: extract operators verbatim, `within` only for numeric ranges, negation patterns map to `!=`.

| # | Snippet | Entity | Expected | Baseline | operator_rules |
|---|---------|--------|----------|----------|----------------|
| 1 | "body mass index **>44** kg/m2" | Body Mass Index | `>` | `<` (inverted!) | `<` (still inverted) |
| 2 | "ASA physical status **1, 2, or 3**" | ASA physical status | `=` | `within` (wrong — it's a discrete list, not a range) | `=` (fixed!) |
| 3 | "**non-pregnant** females" | Pregnancy | `!=` | `!=` (already correct) | `!=` (correct) |

---

## Summary

**3 of the 6 "code failures" are golden data bugs**, not model failures:
- eGFR golden code C0858118 is "Plasma creatinine increased"
- Radiologic examination golden code C0034693 is "Rattus norvegicus"
- GBA gene mutation golden code C4225361 is "Developmental and Epileptic Encephalopathy 29"

**The model is actually correct on those 3.** Fixing the golden data would bring the real score from 9/15 to 12/15.

**1 real code fix:** `specificity_guard` or `context_emphasis` both fix the Ankylosing Spondylitis over-specification (Juvenile AS → AS). Either variant works individually.

**1 real relation fix:** `operator_rules` fixes ASA physical status (`within` → `=` for discrete categorical lists).

**1 remaining stubborn failure:** BMI operator inversion (`>` → `<`) persists across all variants.

**Recommended actions:**
1. Fix 3 golden codes in `test_snippets.json` (eGFR, Radiologic exam, GBA)
2. Apply `specificity_guard` to production system prompt (fixes AS, minimal risk)
3. Apply `operator_rules` to production field mapper (fixes ASA)
4. Investigate BMI inversion separately — may need a few-shot example

Perfect! Now I have all the information. Let me compile a comprehensive report on the MLflow configuration and tracing setup.

## MLflow Configuration & Tracing in MedGemma Hackathon

### 1. **Tracking URI & Storage**

**Configuration:**
- **Environment Variable:** `MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI=http://localhost:5001` (default)
  - From `.env`: Line 69
  - From `.env.example`: Line 75
  
**Storage Backend:**
- **Local Development:** SQLite database at `.mlflow/mlflow.db` with artifacts in `.mlflow/artifacts/`
  - Launched via: `mlflow server --host 0.0.0.0 --port 5001 --backend-store-uri sqlite:///.mlflow/mlflow.db --artifacts-destination .mlflow/artifacts`
  - See Makefile line 79, 94, 99

- **Production/Docker:** Configured via `infra/docker-compose.yml` (not shown, but referenced)

---

### 2. **Trace Structure & Span Naming**

**Trace Organization:**
Each pipeline node creates an **independent top-level trace** (not nested). This is critical for real-time trace appearance.

**Span Names (Node traces):**
- `ingest_node` - Fetch PDF bytes from GCS/local storage
- `extract_node` - Extract structured criteria from PDF
- `parse_node` - Parse extraction JSON into DB records
- `ground_node` - Ground entities to terminology (UMLS, SNOMED, etc.)
- `structure_node` - Infer relational operators and values
- `ordinal_resolve_node` - Resolve ordinal expressions
- `persist_node` - Finalize and persist results

**Child LLM Spans (within nodes):**
- `gemini_extraction` - Gemini extraction calls
- `medgemma_decompose` - MedGemma entity decomposition
- Custom LLM spans for specific tool calls

**Example from ingest_node** (`/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ingest.py`):
```python
with pipeline_span("ingest_node", protocol_id=state.get("protocol_id", "")) as span:
    span.set_inputs({
        "protocol_id": state.get("protocol_id", ""),
        "file_uri": state.get("file_uri", ""),
    })
    # ... processing ...
    span.set_outputs({"pdf_bytes_len": len(pdf_bytes), "status": "processing"})
```

---

### 3. **Trace Tags (Attributes for Filtering)**

**Grouping tags set on every trace:**

| Tag | Value | Usage |
|-----|-------|-------|
| `protocol_id` | Protocol ID string | Filter all runs for a given protocol |
| `run_id` | `{protocol_id}:{uuid4}` | Filter all node traces from a single pipeline invocation |
| `node` | Node name (e.g., `"ingest_node"`) | Filter traces by specific node |

**Example filter in MLflow UI or API:**
```
tags.protocol_id = 'proto-123'       # All runs for protocol-123
tags.run_id = 'proto-123:abc-def-...'  # Single pipeline invocation
tags.node = 'ground_node'             # Only grounding node traces
```

---

### 4. **Span Attributes (Inputs & Outputs)**

**Node-Level Span:**
```python
# Inputs: what was processed
span.set_inputs({
    "protocol_id": "...",
    "file_uri": "gs://bucket/file.pdf",
    # ... other state fields ...
})

# Outputs: what was produced
span.set_outputs({
    "pdf_bytes_len": 12345,
    "status": "processing",
    # ... or error info ...
})
```

**LLM Span (child):**
```python
# LLM Span Context helper in tracing.py
with llm_span("gemini_extraction", model_name="gemini-2.5-flash") as ctx:
    ctx.set_request(prompt_text)  # Truncated to 10,000 chars
    ctx.set_response(response_text, usage={"input_tokens": N, "output_tokens": M})
```

---

### 5. **How Traces Are Created & Flushed**

**Initialization (in `trigger.py`):**

1. **Experiment Setup** (lines 92-104):
   - One experiment per day: `protocol-processing-YYYYMMDD`
   - Prevents experiment ID collision across restarts

2. **Context Variables** (in `tracing.py`):
   - `_pipeline_run_id` - Set once per pipeline invocation
   - `_pipeline_experiment_id` - Set after MLflow initialization

3. **Per-Node Trace Creation** (lines 251-288):
   - `set_pipeline_run_id(thread_id)` called before `graph.ainvoke()`
   - Each node's `pipeline_span()` creates independent trace with tags
   - **Important:** `mlflow.langchain.autolog()` is explicitly NOT enabled (line 273)
     - Autolog wraps entire pipeline in single trace that only appears after completion
     - Without autolog, each span flushes immediately to MLflow

---

### 6. **Querying & Viewing Traces**

**MLflow Client API** (from `harvest_failures.py` lines 259-324):

```python
import mlflow
client = mlflow.MlflowClient()

# Search traces by protocol_id tag
traces = client.search_traces(
    filter_string="tags.protocol_id = 'proto-123'",
    max_results=20,
)

# Access trace data
for t in traces:
    tags = t.info.tags  # Dict of tags set on trace
    spans = t.data.spans  # List of spans in the trace
    
    for span in spans:
        span_name = span.name        # "ingest_node", etc.
        span_status = span.status    # "OK", "ERROR", "IN_PROGRESS"
        inputs = span.inputs         # Dict or None
        outputs = span.outputs       # Dict or None
```

**Web UI:**
- Access at configured `MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI` (default: `http://localhost:5001`)
- Filter by tags, experiment, date range
- View full trace lineage and individual span details

**Cleanup/Export Tools:**

| Script | Purpose |
|--------|---------|
| `scripts/clear_mlflow_traces.py` | Delete all runs/traces from tracking server |
| `scripts/verify_mlflow_tracing.py` | Verify per-node tracing works (independent traces, tags, real-time appearance) |
| `scripts/harvest_failures.py` | Query failed protocols + traces, generate golden test snippets with Gemini |

---

### 7. **Complete Trace Lifecycle Example**

From `verify_mlflow_tracing.py` (lines 126-211):

```
PIPELINE RUN START
├─ node_a creates trace "node_a" with tags: {protocol_id, run_id, node}
│  ├─ Trace flushed to MLflow immediately (span context exits)
│  └─ Next invocation sees count_traces() = 1
├─ node_b creates trace "node_b" with same tags
│  ├─ Trace flushed immediately
│  └─ count_traces() = 2
└─ node_c creates trace "node_c" with same tags
   ├─ Trace flushed immediately
   └─ count_traces() = 3

RESULT: 3 independent traces visible in real-time (not batched)
```

---

### 8. **Key Configuration Files**

| File | Lines | Purpose |
|------|-------|---------|
| `.env` | 69-70 | `MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI` setting |
| `.env.example` | 74-75 | MLflow documentation in defaults |
| `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tracing.py` | 1-225 | Core tracing module with `pipeline_span()` and `llm_span()` context managers |
| `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/trigger.py` | 35-149, 251-289 | MLflow initialization, experiment setup, orphan trace cleanup |
| `scripts/verify_mlflow_tracing.py` | 1-212 | Test suite for trace behavior |
| `scripts/clear_mlflow_traces.py` | 1-98 | Trace deletion utility |
| `scripts/harvest_failures.py` | 259-324 | Example: query traces for failure analysis |
| `Makefile` | 37-99, 179-181 | MLflow server launch and cleanup commands |

---

### 9. **Running MLflow**

**Local Development:**
```bash
make run-mlflow              # Standalone MLflow on :5001
make run-infra               # DB + MLflow
make run-dev                 # Full stack (DB, API, UI, MLflow)
```

**Cleanup:**
```bash
make mlflow-clear            # Delete all runs/traces
```

**Verify Tracing:**
```bash
uv run python scripts/verify_mlflow_tracing.py  # Test real-time behavior
```

This comprehensive setup ensures that traces are captured in real-time, grouped by protocol and run ID for easy filtering, and stored persistently in SQLite locally (or a production backend for Docker).

```
     1→"""MLflow tracing helpers for pipeline nodes.
     2→
     3→Provides a safe context manager that creates MLflow traces when available
     4→and falls back to a no-op when MLflow is not configured or installed.
     5→
     6→Each node creates its own separate top-level trace (not a child span),
     7→tagged with protocol_id and run_id so traces from the same pipeline run
     8→can be grouped and filtered in the MLflow UI.
     9→
    10→IMPORTANT: ``mlflow.langchain.autolog()`` must NOT be enabled.  Autolog
    11→wraps ``graph.ainvoke()`` in a single parent trace and all node spans
    12→become children — producing one constantly-updating trace that only
    13→appears after the full pipeline finishes.  Without autolog, each call to
    14→``mlflow.start_span()`` inside a node creates an independent root trace
    15→that is flushed to MLflow as soon as the context manager exits.
    16→
    17→Grouping tags set on every trace:
    18→    protocol_id  – groups all runs for a given protocol
    19→    run_id       – groups all node traces from the same pipeline invocation
    20→    node         – the node name (for easy filtering)
    21→"""
    22→
    23→from __future__ import annotations
    24→
    25→import contextvars
    26→import logging
    27→import os
    28→from contextlib import contextmanager
    29→from typing import Any, Generator, cast
    30→
    31→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    32→
    33→# ContextVar set once per pipeline invocation in trigger._run_pipeline().
    34→# Every node's pipeline_span() reads it to tag traces with a shared run_id.
    35→_pipeline_run_id: contextvars.ContextVar[str] = contextvars.ContextVar(
    36→    "pipeline_run_id", default=""
    37→)
    38→
    39→# Experiment ID for pipeline traces. Set by trigger after _ensure_mlflow() so
    40→# spans carry the experiment ID; export then uses it even from another thread.
    41→_pipeline_experiment_id: contextvars.ContextVar[str | None] = contextvars.ContextVar(
    42→    "pipeline_experiment_id", default=None
    43→)
    44→
    45→
    46→def set_pipeline_run_id(run_id: str) -> None:
    47→    """Set the current pipeline run_id (call before graph.ainvoke)."""
    48→    _pipeline_run_id.set(run_id)
    49→
    50→
    51→def set_pipeline_experiment_id(experiment_id: str | None) -> None:
    52→    """Set experiment ID for pipeline traces (after _ensure_mlflow in trigger)."""
    53→    _pipeline_experiment_id.set(experiment_id)
    54→
    55→
    56→@contextmanager
    57→def pipeline_span(
    58→    name: str,
    59→    span_type: str = "CHAIN",
    60→    protocol_id: str = "",
    61→) -> Generator[Any, None, None]:
    62→    """Create a separate MLflow trace for a pipeline node.
    63→
    64→    Each call creates its own top-level trace tagged with protocol_id
    65→    and run_id, so individual node traces appear in MLflow as they
    66→    complete rather than waiting for the entire pipeline to finish.
    67→
    68→    Filter in MLflow UI:
    69→        tags.protocol_id = "<id>"       – all runs for a protocol
    70→        tags.run_id      = "<run_id>"   – single pipeline invocation
    71→
    72→    Args:
    73→        name: Trace/span name (e.g., "ingest_node", "ground_node").
    74→        span_type: MLflow span type (default "CHAIN").
    75→        protocol_id: Protocol ID to tag the trace with for session grouping.
    76→
    77→    Yields:
    78→        MLflow Span object or a no-op wrapper.
    79→    """
    80→    try:
    81→        import mlflow
    82→        from mlflow.entities.trace_location import MlflowExperimentLocation
    83→
    84→        if os.getenv("MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI"):
    85→            # Pass experiment_id on span so export uses it even from another thread.
    86→            exp_id = _pipeline_experiment_id.get()
    87→            trace_destination = (
    88→                MlflowExperimentLocation(experiment_id=exp_id) if exp_id else None
    89→            )
    90→            with mlflow.start_span(
    91→                name=name,
    92→                span_type=span_type,
    93→                trace_destination=trace_destination,
    94→            ) as span:
    95→                # Tag the trace for grouping/filtering in the MLflow UI.
    96→                tags: dict[str, str] = {"node": name}
    97→                if protocol_id:
    98→                    tags["protocol_id"] = protocol_id
    99→                run_id = _pipeline_run_id.get()
   100→                if run_id:
   101→                    tags["run_id"] = run_id
   102→                try:
   103→                    mlflow.update_current_trace(tags=tags)
   104→                except Exception:
   105→                    logger.warning("MLflow tag update failed", exc_info=True)
   106→                yield cast(Any, span)
   107→                return
   108→    except ImportError:
   109→        logger.warning("mlflow not installed — tracing disabled")
   110→    except Exception:
   111→        logger.warning(
   112→            "MLflow span creation failed, falling back to no-op", exc_info=True
   113→        )
   114→
   115→    # Fallback: no-op span
   116→    yield cast(Any, _NoOpSpan())
   117→
   118→
   119→class _NoOpSpan:
   120→    """No-op span that silently accepts MLflow span API calls."""
   121→
   122→    def set_inputs(self, inputs: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   123→        pass
   124→
   125→    def set_outputs(self, outputs: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   126→        pass
   127→
   128→    def set_status(self, status: str) -> None:
   129→        pass
   130→
   131→
   132→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   133→# LLM-level child span helpers
   134→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   135→
   136→_TRUNCATE_LEN = 10_000
   137→
   138→
   139→def _truncate(text: str, max_len: int = _TRUNCATE_LEN) -> str:
   140→    if len(text) <= max_len:
   141→        return text
   142→    return text[:max_len] + f"... [truncated, {len(text)} total chars]"
   143→
   144→
   145→class _LLMSpanCtx:
   146→    """Wraps an MLflow span and exposes set_request / set_response helpers."""
   147→
   148→    def __init__(self, span: Any, model_name: str) -> None:
   149→        self._span = span
   150→        self._model_name = model_name
   151→        self._finalized = False
   152→
   153→    def set_request(self, prompt_text: str) -> None:
   154→        """Record the LLM request (prompt + model) as span inputs."""
   155→        self._span.set_inputs(
   156→            {
   157→                "model": self._model_name,
   158→                "prompt": _truncate(prompt_text),
   159→            }
   160→        )
   161→
   162→    def set_response(
   163→        self,
   164→        response_text: str,
   165→        usage: dict[str, int] | None = None,
   166→    ) -> None:
   167→        """Record the LLM response and optional token counts as span outputs."""
   168→        outputs: dict[str, Any] = {"response": _truncate(response_text)}
   169→        if usage:
   170→            outputs.update(usage)
   171→        self._span.set_outputs(outputs)
   172→        self._finalized = True
   173→
   174→    def _finalize(self) -> None:
   175→        """Ensure outputs are set even if set_response was never called."""
   176→        if not self._finalized:
   177→            self._span.set_outputs({"response": "(not captured)"})
   178→
   179→
   180→class _NoOpLLMSpan:
   181→    """No-op replacement when MLflow is unavailable."""
   182→
   183→    def set_request(self, prompt_text: str) -> None:
   184→        pass
   185→
   186→    def set_response(
   187→        self,
   188→        response_text: str,
   189→        usage: dict[str, int] | None = None,
   190→    ) -> None:
   191→        pass
   192→
   193→
   194→@contextmanager
   195→def llm_span(name: str, model_name: str = "") -> Generator[Any, None, None]:
   196→    """Create an MLflow child span for an individual LLM call.
   197→
   198→    When called inside an active ``pipeline_span()``, this automatically
   199→    becomes a child span (MLflow 3.x nests ``start_span`` calls).
   200→    When MLflow is unavailable the context manager yields a no-op object.
   201→
   202→    Args:
   203→        name: Span name (e.g. ``"gemini_extraction"``).
   204→        model_name: Model identifier to record in span inputs.
   205→
   206→    Yields:
   207→        ``_LLMSpanCtx`` (or ``_NoOpLLMSpan``) with ``set_request`` /
   208→        ``set_response`` helpers.
   209→    """
   210→    try:
   211→        import mlflow
   212→
   213→        if os.getenv("MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI"):
   214→            with mlflow.start_span(name=name, span_type="LLM") as span:
   215→                ctx = _LLMSpanCtx(span, model_name)
   216→                yield cast(Any, ctx)
   217→                ctx._finalize()
   218→                return
   219→    except ImportError:
   220→        pass  # mlflow is an optional dependency; fall through to no-op span
   221→    except Exception:
   222→        logger.debug("llm_span creation failed, falling back to no-op", exc_info=True)
   223→
   224→    yield cast(Any, _NoOpLLMSpan())
   225→
```

  "description": "Query MLflow for recent traces from the harness run",
  "timeout": 30000
}

{
  "command": "set -a && source .env && set +a && uv run python3 -c \"\nimport mlflow\n\nclient = mlflow.MlflowClient()\n\n# Find all medgemma_evaluate traces (these have the prompts + MedGemma responses)\ntraces = client.search_traces(\n    filter_string=\\\"tags.mlflow.traceName = 'medgemma_evaluate'\\\",\n    max_results=100,\n    order_by=['timestamp_ms DESC'],\n)\nprint(f'medgemma_evaluate traces: {len(traces)}')\n\n# Find the ones about Ankylosing Spondylitis by checking inputs\nas_traces = []\nfor t in traces:\n    spans = t.data.spans or []\n    for s in spans:\n        inp = s.inputs or {}\n        prompt = inp.get('prompt', '')\n        if 'Ankylosing' in prompt:\n            as_traces.append(t)\n            # Extract timestamp for ordering\n            print(f'  trace_id={t.info.trace_id[:16]} ts={t.info.timestamp_ms}')\n            break\n\nprint(f'\\\\nAnkylosing Spondylitis traces: {len(as_traces)}')\nprint('(should be 4 — one per variant: baseline, specificity_guard, context_emphasis, combined)')\n\" 2>/dev/null\n",
  "description": "Find Ankylosing Spondylitis traces across all 4 variants",
  "timeout": 30000
}

```json
{
  "command": "set -a && source .env && set +a && uv run python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport mlflow\n\nclient = mlflow.MlflowClient()\n\n# Get all recent traces, then filter locally\ntraces = client.search_traces(\n    max_results=200,\n    order_by=['timestamp_ms DESC'],\n)\nprint(f\"Total recent traces: {len(traces)}\")\n\n# Filter for medgemma_evaluate traces about Ankylosing Spondylitis\nas_traces = []\nfor t in traces:\n    tags = t.info.tags or {}\n    trace_name = tags.get(\"mlflow.traceName\", \"\")\n    if trace_name != \"medgemma_evaluate\":\n        continue\n    spans = t.data.spans or []\n    for s in spans:\n        inp = s.inputs or {}\n        prompt = inp.get(\"prompt\", \"\")\n        if \"Ankylosing\" in prompt:\n            as_traces.append((t, s))\n            break\n\nprint(f\"Ankylosing Spondylitis medgemma_evaluate traces: {len(as_traces)}\")\nprint(\"(expect 4: baseline, specificity_guard, context_emphasis, combined)\\n\")\n\n# Print each one — show the system prompt diff and MedGemma's response\nfor i, (t, s) in enumerate(reversed(as_traces)):  # oldest first\n    inp = s.inputs or {}\n    out = s.outputs or {}\n    prompt = inp.get(\"prompt\", \"\")\n    response = out.get(\"response\", \"\")\n\n    # Detect which variant by checking for variant-specific text\n    if \"9. When the entity text\" in prompt and \"Cross-check\" in prompt:\n        variant = \"combined\"\n    elif \"9. When the entity text\" in prompt:\n        variant = \"specificity_guard\"\n    elif \"Cross-check\" in prompt:\n        variant = \"context_emphasis\"\n    else:\n        variant = \"baseline\"\n\n    print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n    print(f\"VARIANT {i+1}: {variant}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n    print(f\"Timestamp: {t.info.timestamp_ms}\")\n    print(f\"\\n--- MedGemma Response ---\")\n    print(response[:2000])\n    print()\nPYEOF\n",
  "description": "Extract and compare MedGemma responses for AS across all 4 variants",
  "timeout": 30000
}
```

Prompt:
<start_of_turn>user
You are a medical terminology expert. Your task is to select the best terminology code for a medical entity extracted from a clinical trial eligibility criterion.

You will be given:
- The entity text and type
- The criterion context it came from
- A list of terminology candidates from medical APIs (UMLS, SNOMED, RxNorm, etc.)

Your job: Select the single best match or determine no match exists.

## Confidence Scoring

- **0.9-1.0**: Exact match — direct preferred term or recognized synonym
- **0.7-0.8**: Close synonym — different wording, same clinical concept
- **0.5-0.6**: Partial match — related but broader/narrower concept
- **0.0**: No match — wrong domain, not medical, or contradicts context

## Rules

1. Prefer exact or near-exact concept matches over broad categories
2. For medications, prefer RxNorm over UMLS when both are available
3. For conditions and procedures, UMLS or SNOMED is appropriate
4. If the entity is a number, unit, or procedural language (not a medical concept), set confidence=0.0
5. Always provide reasoning for your selection
6. Output your analysis in plain text — the output will be structured by a downstream system
7. Text overlap is NOT sufficient for a match. The candidate must represent the same CLINICAL MEANING as the entity in the criterion context. Candidates from the wrong clinical domain (e.g., medical devices or supplies when the entity is a clinical procedure or condition) must receive confidence 0.0 regardles

get enough to cover our run\n\nfor i, (t, s) in enumerate(recent_struct):\n    inp = s.inputs or {}\n    out = s.outputs or {}\n    prompt = inp.get(\"prompt\", \"\")\n    response = out.get(\"response\", \"\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n--- Struct trace {i+1} (ts={t.info.timestamp_ms}) ---\")\n    # The prompt to gemini_structure_decision contains the MedGemma raw output\n    # Show just the analysis part (after the echoed prompt, if any)\n    # Look for the actual analysis/selection text\n    for marker in [\"Best match:\", \"best match\", \"Selected:\", \"I select\", \"**Best\", \"**Selected\", \"Candidate\", \"Analysis\"]:\n        idx = prompt.lower().find(marker.lower())\n        if idx >= 0:\n            print(prompt[max(0,idx-50):idx+500])\n            break\n    else:\n        # }

Our run had 6 entities x 4 variants = 24 medgemma calls\n# AS is entity 3 in each variant pass\n\n# Get the 4 most recent AS eval+struct pairs\nrecent_evals = as_evals[:4]  # combined, context_emphasis, specificity_guard, baseline (reverse order)\nrecent_structs = as_structs[:4]\n\nfor i, ((te, se), (ts_t, ss)) in enumerate(zip(recent_evals, recent_structs)):\n    inp = se.inputs or {}\n    out_eval = se.outputs or {}\n    out_struct = ss.outputs or {}\n\n    prompt = inp.get(\"prompt\", \"\")\n    medgemma_response = out_eval.get(\"response\", \"\")\n    gemini_output = out_struct.get(\"response\", \"\")\n\n    # Detect variant\n    has_rules = \"9. When the entity text\" in prompt\n    has_crosscheck = \"Cross-check\" in prompt\n    if has_rules and has_crosscheck:\n        variant = \"combined\"\n    elif has_rules:\n        variant = \"specificity_guard\"\n    elif has_crosscheck:\n        variant = \"context_emphasis\"\n    else:\n        variant = \"baseline\"\n\n    # Extract MedGemma's actual analysis (after <start_of_turn>model or the prompt echo)\n    model_output = medgemma_response\n    if \"<start_of_turn>model\" in model_output:\n        model_output = model_output.split(\"<start_of_turn>model\")[-1]\n    # Sometimes it starts with \"Output:\" prefix\n    if \"Output:\" in model_output:\n        model_output = model_output[model_output.index(\"Output:\"):]\n\n    print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n    print(f\"VARIANT: {variant}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n    print(f\"\\n--- MedGemma Reasoning (after prompt echo) ---\")\n    print(model_output[:2000].strip())\n    print(f\"\\n--- Gemini Final Decision ---\")\n    print(gemini_output[:400])\n    print()\nPYEOF\n",
  "description": "Extract full MedGemma reasoning for AS across all 4 variants",
  "timeout": 30000
}

{
  "command": "set -a && source .env && set +a && uv run python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport mlflow, warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings(\"ignore\")\n\nclient = mlflow.MlflowClient()\ntraces = client.search_traces(\n    experiment_ids=[\"0\"],\n    max_results=300,\n    order_by=[\"timestamp_ms DESC\"],\n)\n\n# Get ALL AS gemini_structure_decision traces with full context\nas_struct = []\nfor t in traces:\n    tags = t.info.tags or {}\n    if tags.get(\"mlflow.traceName\") != \"gemini_structure_decision\":\n        continue\n    spans = t.data.spans or []\n    for s in spans:\n        inp = s.inputs or {}\n        out = s.outputs or {}\n        prompt = inp.get(\"prompt\", \"\")\n        resp = out.get(\"response\", \"\")\n        # The prompt to gemini_structure IS the raw MedGemma output\n        if (\"C0038013\" in resp or \"C0409675\" in resp) and \"spondylitis\" in prompt.lower():\n            as_struct.append((t, s, resp, prompt))\n            break\n\n# Show the 8 most recent (covers our 4 variants + some prior runs)\nfor i, (t, s, resp, raw_medgemma) in enumerate(as_struct[:8]):\n    # Extract code from response\n    code = \"C0038013\" if \"C0038013\" in resp else \"C0409675\"\n    \n    # Get the MedGemma analysis portion\n    analysis = raw_medgemma\n    if \"<start_of_turn>model\" in analysis:\n        analysis = analysis.split(\"<start_of_turn>model\")[-1]\n    \n    # Trim to just the decision part\n    analysis = analysis.strip()[:1200]\n    \n    print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n    print(f\"TRACE {i+1} (ts={t.info.timestamp_ms}) => {code}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*80}\")\n    print(analysis)\n    print()\nPYEOF\n",
  "description": "Get the full MedGemma analysis for each AS decision",
  "timeout": 30000
}

{
  "command": "set -a && source .env && set +a && uv run python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport mlflow, warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings(\"ignore\")\n\nclient = mlflow.MlflowClient()\ntraces = client.search_traces(\n    experiment_ids=[\"0\"],\n    max_results=300,\n    order_by=[\"timestamp_ms DESC\"],\n)\n\n# Get the medgemma_evaluate traces for AS, show the CANDIDATES section from the prompt\nas_evals = []\nfor t in traces:\n    tags = t.info.tags or {}\n    if tags.get(\"mlflow.traceName\") != \"medgemma_evaluate\":\n        continue\n    spans = t.data.spans or []\n    for s in spans:\n        inp = s.inputs or {}\n        prompt = inp.get(\"prompt\", \"\")\n        if \"Ankylosing\" in prompt:\n            as_evals.append((t, s, prompt))\n            break\n\n# Show the candidate list from the most recent trace (same across all variants)\n_, _, prompt = as_evals[0]\n# Extract the candidates section between \"## Candidates\" and \"## Instructions\"\nif \"## Candidates\" in prompt and \"## Instructions\" in prompt:\n    start = prompt.index(\"## Candidates\")\n    end = prompt.index(\"## Instructions\")\n    candidates_section = prompt[start:end]\n    # Show just the candidates with C0038013 or C0409675\n    print(\"=== CANDIDATES CONTAINING C0038013 OR C0409675 ===\")\n    for line in candidates_section.split(\"\\n\"):\n        if \"C0038013\" in line or \"C0409675\" in line:\n            print(line.strip())\n    print()\n    \n    # Count total candidates\n    cand_lines = [l for l in candidates_section.split(\"\\n\") if l.strip().startswith(\"- Source:\")]\n    print(f\"Total candidates: {len(cand_lines)}\")\n    \n    # Show top 5 by score\n    print(\"\\n=== TOP 5 CANDIDATES BY SCORE ===\")\n    for line in cand_lines[:5]:\n        print(line.strip())\nPYEOF\n",
  "description": "Show candidate list for AS to see both C0038013 and C0409675",
  "timeout": 15000
}

Now I can see the full picture. Here's the diagnosis:

**C0038013 "Ankylosing spondylitis" is in the candidates at Score: 1.00** (both SNOMED and UMLS). C0409675 "Juvenile ankylosing spondylitis" is at Score: 0.90. So MedGemma has the exact match available but **chooses the lower-scored Juvenile variant anyway** in the `combined` prompt.

The problem: when both `specificity_guard` (rules 9-10 in system prompt) and `context_emphasis` (cross-check in evaluate) are combined, the prompt becomes too long and the model loses focus. It acknowledges the over-specificity ("does not contain qualifiers like 'Juvenile'") but still picks it.

Now let me create a new focused variant and test it.

Now I'll add the new variants based on the trace analysis. The key insight: `combined` fails because 2 rule sets in different locations (system + evaluate) create noise — MedGemma sees the rule, acknowledges it, then ignores it. The fix: one short, mandatory, procedural instruction in the evaluate template only.

> TOOL

  "new_string": "# Diagnosis from MLflow traces: the `combined` variant fails because stacking\n# rules in BOTH system + evaluate overloads the model — it acknowledges the\n# rules then ignores them. The `exact_match_first` variant puts ONE concise\n# procedural instruction in the evaluate template: scan for exact matches\n# first, only fall through to subspecific candidates if none found.\n_EXACT_MATCH_FIRST_EVALUATE = (\n    \"\\n\\n## MANDATORY — Exact-Match-First Protocol\\n\"\n    \"BEFORE analyzing candidates, follow this procedure:\\n\"\n    \"1. Scan the candidate list for any entry whose preferred_term matches \"\n    \"the entity text exactly (case-insensitive, ignoring minor word order).\\n\"\n    \"2. If one or more exact matches exist, select the one with the highest \"\n    \"score. Do NOT consider subspecific variants that add qualifiers \"\n    \"(e.g., 'Juvenile', 'Chronic', region-specific) when an exact match \"\n    \"is available.\\n\"\n    \"3. Only if NO exact match exists, proceed to analyze close synonyms.\"\n)\n\n# ── Field mapper variant fragments ──────────────────────────────────────────"
}

The harness completed. Here are the full results:

## Code Selection (MedGemma variants) — 6 entities

| Entity | Expected | baseline | specificity_guard | context_emphasis | combined | exact_match_first |
|--------|----------|----------|-------------------|------------------|----------|-------------------|
| Serum creatinine | C0201976 | OK | OK | OK | OK | **C0600061** |
| eGFR | C3811844 | OK | OK | OK | OK | OK |
| Ankylosing Spondylitis | C0038013 | **C0409675** | OK | **C0409675** | OK | OK |
| Radiologic examination | C0043299 | OK | OK | OK | OK | OK |
| GBA gene mutation | C3888963 | OK | OK | OK | OK | OK |
| Pregnancy | C0032961 | OK | OK | OK | OK | OK |
| **Totals** | | **5/6** | **6/6** | **5/6** | **6/6** | **5/6** |

## Relation Extraction (Field mapper variants) — 3 entities

| Entity | Expected | baseline | operator_rules | operator_rules_v2 |
|--------|----------|----------|----------------|--------------------|
| Body Mass Index | `>` | `<` | `<` | `<` |
| ASA physical status | `=` | `<=` | OK | OK |
| Pregnancy | `!=` | `=` | OK | OK |
| **Totals** | | **0/3** | **2/3** | **2/3** |

## Analysis

**Code selection winners: `specificity_guard` and `combined` (both 6/6)**

- `specificity_guard` is the better choice — it only adds rules to the system prompt and achieves perfect results. `combined` stacks rules in both system + evaluate, which is more fragile (MLflow traces showed the model sometimes acknowledges then ignores stacked rules).
- `exact_match_first` backfired — it introduced a NEW regression on Serum creatinine (picked C0600061 instead of C0201976), likely because the exact-match-first protocol is too rigid and matched a wrong candidate with a close preferred term.

**Relation extraction winner: `operator_rules` (2/3, tied with v2)**

- Both fix ASA (`<=` → `=`) and Pregnancy (`=` → `!=`).
- The BMI operator inversion (`>` → `<`) persists across ALL variants, including `operator_rules_v2` with its explicit worked example. The LLM consistently interprets "body mass index >44" as an upper-limit exclusion and flips the operator.

**Best combination: `specificity_guard` + `operator_rules` = 8/9 on tested entities (14/15 on full pipeline)**

The BMI operator inversion is the one remaining failure. It appears to be a deep LLM reasoning bias — the model "understands" that BMI >44 in a clinical trial context means excluding heavy patients, so it outputs `<` (as in "BMI must be less than 44"). Even an explicit worked example with the exact failing text couldn't override this. Fixing it likely requires either post-processing logic or a fundamentally different prompting approach (e.g., two-step: first extract the raw text span, then parse the operator from the extracted span).

Want me to apply the winning variants (`specificity_guard` + `operator_rules`) to the production prompts?

    13→- **0.7-0.8**: Close synonym — different wording, same clinical concept
    14→- **0.5-0.6**: Partial match — related but broader/narrower concept
    15→- **0.0**: No match — wrong domain, not medical, or contradicts context

    17→## Rules
    18→

    20→2. For medications, prefer RxNorm over UMLS when both are available

    25→7. Text overlap is NOT sufficient for a match. The candidate must represent the same CLINICAL MEANING as the entity in the criterion context. Candidates from the wrong clinical domain (e.g., medical devices or supplies when the entity is a clinical procedure or condition) must receive confidence 0.0 regardless of word overlap.
