```
     1→"""Field mapping generation tool for grounded entities.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "Generate suggested field_mappings during grounding (ground node)"
     4→Per CONTEXT.md: "Criteria should be decomposed per AutoCriteria pattern: separate
     5→Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time"
     6→Per user decision: "MedGemma and Gemini collaborate: Gemini uses MedGemma as
     7→medical expert"
     8→
     9→Uses Gemini to generate suggested field mappings for the grounded entity +
    10→criterion text. These are best-effort suggestions — reviewer can edit in UI.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import logging
    16→from typing import Any, Literal
    17→
    18→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator, model_validator
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    21→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
    22→    create_structured_llm,
    23→    parse_structured_output,
    24→)
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→# Mapping from legacy/LLM relation operators to the frontend's RelationOperator set
    29→_RELATION_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    30→    "has": "contains",
    31→    "is": "=",
    32→    "not": "not_contains",
    33→    "==": "=",
    34→    "range": "within",
    35→}
    36→
    37→
    38→def _normalize_relation(rel: str) -> str:
    39→    """Normalize a relation operator to the frontend's accepted set.
    40→
    41→    Maps legacy operators (has, is, not, ==, range) to the standard set:
    42→    =, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within, not_in_last, contains, not_contains.
    43→    """
    44→    return _RELATION_MAP.get(rel, rel)
    45→
    46→
    47→class FieldMappingValue(BaseModel):
    48→    """Typed value object for field mappings with type discriminator.
    49→
    50→    Supports three value shapes:
    51→    - standard: single value + unit (e.g. HbA1c < 7%)
    52→    - range: min/max + unit (e.g. Age 18-65 years)
    53→    - temporal: duration + unit (e.g. within 6 months)
    54→    """
    55→
    56→    type: Literal["standard", "range", "temporal"] = Field(
    57→        description="Value type discriminator"
    58→    )
    59→    value: str | None = Field(
    60→        default=None, description="Value for standard type (e.g. '7')"
    61→    )
    62→    unit: str | None = Field(
    63→        default=None, description="Unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'months')"
    64→    )
    65→    min: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Minimum value for range type")
    66→    max: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Maximum value for range type")
    67→    duration: str | None = Field(
    68→        default=None, description="Duration value for temporal type"
    69→    )
    70→
    71→    @model_validator(mode="after")
    72→    def truncate_long_strings(self) -> "FieldMappingValue":
    73→        """Guard against LLM repetition loops producing absurdly long values."""
    74→        _max = 200
    75→        for attr in ("value", "unit", "min", "max", "duration"):
    76→            val = getattr(self, attr)
    77→            if isinstance(val, str) and len(val) > _max:
    78→                setattr(self, attr, val[:_max])
    79→        return self
    80→
    81→
    82→# The set of valid relation operators accepted by the frontend
    83→RelationOperator = Literal[
    84→    "=", "!=", ">", ">=", "<", "<=", "within", "not_in_last", "contains", "not_contains"
    85→]
    86→
    87→
    88→class FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):
    89→    """A single AutoCriteria field mapping decomposition.
    90→
    91→    Per the AutoCriteria pattern, each criterion is decomposed into
    92→    separate Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, and Time components.
    93→    """
    94→
    95→    entity: str = Field(description="The medical entity name (e.g. 'HbA1c')")
    96→    relation: RelationOperator = Field(
    97→        description="The logical operator/relation (e.g. '<', '>', '=', 'contains')"
    98→    )
    99→    value: FieldMappingValue = Field(
   100→        description="Typed value object with type discriminator"
   101→    )
   102→    unit: str | None = Field(
   103→        default=None,
   104→        description="Optional unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')",
   105→    )
   106→    value_concept_id: str | None = Field(
   107→        default=None,
   108→        description="OMOP concept ID for categorical values",
   109→    )
   110→    value_concept_system: str | None = Field(
   111→        default=None,
   112→        description="Terminology system for value_concept_id (e.g. 'SNOMED', 'OMOP')",
   113→    )
   114→
   115→    @field_validator("relation", mode="before")
   116→    @classmethod
   117→    def normalize_relation(cls, v: str) -> str:
   118→        """Normalize LLM-generated relation operators before Literal validation."""
   119→        if isinstance(v, str):
   120→            return _normalize_relation(v)
   121→        return v
   122→
   123→
   124→class FieldMappingResponse(BaseModel):
   125→    """Gemini structured output for field mappings."""
   126→
   127→    mappings: list[FieldMappingItem] = Field(
   128→        default_factory=list,
   129→        description="List of AutoCriteria field mapping decompositions",
   130→    )
   131→
   132→
   133→async def generate_field_mappings(
   134→    entity: EntityGroundingResult,
   135→    criterion_text: str,
   136→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   137→    """Generate suggested field mappings for a grounded entity.
   138→
   139→    Uses Gemini to decompose the criterion text into AutoCriteria field mappings:
   140→    Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time components for each discrete condition.
   141→
   142→    This is a best-effort suggestion — reviewer can edit in the UI. Errors
   143→    are logged and an empty list returned (not propagated as failures).
   144→
   145→    Args:
   146→        entity: Grounded EntityGroundingResult with code and preferred term.
   147→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   148→
   149→    Returns:
   150→        List of field mapping dicts with keys: entity, relation, value,
   151→        entity_code, entity_system, omop_concept_id, entity_type.
   152→        Empty list if generation fails.
   153→    """
   154→    if not criterion_text:
   155→        return []
   156→
   157→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)
   158→    if structured_llm is None:
   159→        return []
   160→
   161→    try:
   162→        # Build a context-rich prompt for field mapping generation
   163→        grounded_term = entity.preferred_term or entity.entity_text
   164→        code_context = ""
   165→        if entity.selected_code and entity.selected_system:
   166→            system = entity.selected_system.upper()
   167→            code_context = f"(grounded to {system} code: {entity.selected_code})"
   168→
   169→        prompt = (
   170→            "<role>Clinical trial protocol analyst. Decompose criterion"
   171→            " into Entity-Relation-Value-Unit field mappings.</role>\n\n"
   172→            "<entity_context>\n"
   173→            f"Medical entity: {grounded_term} {code_context}\n"
   174→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n"
   175→            "</entity_context>\n\n"
   176→            "<rules>\n"
   177→            "- Extract each discrete measurement, threshold, or condition"
   178→            " as a separate mapping\n"
   179→            "- relation: one of =, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within,"
   180→            " not_in_last, contains, not_contains\n"
   181→            "- Boolean normalization: presence → relation='=', value='True';"
   182→            " absence → relation='!=', value='True'."
   183→            " Never use 'present'/'absent'/'confirmed' as values.\n"
   184→            "- value types: standard {value, unit},"
   185→            " range {min, max, unit}, temporal {duration, unit}\n"
   186→            "- Temporal duration MUST be a plain number."
   187→            ' "within 5 days" → duration="5", unit="days"\n'
   188→            "- Default: if no measurement, use relation='=',"
   189→            ' value={"type":"standard","value":"True","unit":null}\n'
   190→            "- Relative value resolution: when the criterion expresses"
   191→            " a threshold relative to a reference range or normal limit,"
   192→            " you MUST resolve it to an absolute numeric value with a"
   193→            " standard unit. Use your medical knowledge of the entity's"
   194→            " reference range, compute the absolute threshold, and return"
   195→            " only the computed number in the value field and the standard"
   196→            " lab unit in the unit field. Never return the relative"
   197→            " expression as the value.\n"
   198→            "</rules>\n\n"
   199→            "<anti_patterns>\n"
   200→            "BAD: value='present' → use value='True'\n"
   201→            "BAD: duration='5-days-ago' → use duration='5', unit='days'\n"
   202→            "BAD: value='twice the normal limit' → resolve to absolute\n"
   203→            "</anti_patterns>"
   204→        )
   205→
   206→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   207→
   208→        with llm_span("gemini_field_mapping") as llm:
   209→            llm.set_request(prompt)
   210→            result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   211→            llm.set_response(str(result))
   212→
   213→        response = parse_structured_output(result, FieldMappingResponse)
   214→
   215→        mappings = [
   216→            {
   217→                "entity": m.entity,
   218→                "relation": m.relation,
   219→                "value": m.value.model_dump(exclude_none=True),
   220→                "entity_code": entity.selected_code,
   221→                "entity_system": entity.selected_system,
   222→                "omop_concept_id": entity.omop_concept_id,
   223→                "entity_type": entity.entity_type,
   224→                "value_concept_id": m.value_concept_id,
   225→                "value_concept_system": m.value_concept_system,
   226→            }
   227→            for m in response.mappings
   228→        ]
   229→
   230→        logger.info(
   231→            "Generated %d field mapping(s) for entity '%s'",
   232→            len(mappings),
   233→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   234→        )
   235→        return mappings
   236→
   237→    except Exception as e:
   238→        logger.warning(
   239→            "Field mapping generation failed for entity '%s': %s",
   240→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   241→            e,
   242→            exc_info=True,
   243→        )
   244→        # Best-effort: return empty list on failure
   245→        return []
   246→
```
```

     1→"""ToolUniverse client wrapper for terminology system lookups.
     2→
     3→Provides a module-level singleton ToolUniverse instance with in-memory TTL
     4→caching for autocomplete and pipeline grounding. Exposes a single
     5→`search_terminology(system, query)` function for all 6 medical terminology
     6→systems: UMLS, SNOMED, ICD-10, LOINC, RxNorm, HPO.
     7→
     8→Architecture:
     9→- Singleton via @lru_cache(maxsize=1) on _get_tu()
    10→- Two-layer cache: exact-match TTLCache + prefix-aware autocomplete cache
    11→- Scoped tool loading (6 tools via include_tools) rather than all 1495
    12→- Response parsing handles each system's unique return format
    13→- Retry on transient 502/503 errors (3 attempts, exponential backoff)
    14→- Circuit breaker trips after 10 consecutive failures (60s cooldown)
    15→- Sentence-length queries pre-processed to extract medical terms
    16→
    17→Caching strategy for autocomplete:
    18→  Typing "hyp" → "hype" → "hyper" used to make 3 API calls (different keys).
    19→  Now the prefix cache checks: if we already have results for "hyp" and the
    20→  new query "hype" starts with "hyp", we filter the cached "hyp" results
    21→  client-side by checking if preferred_term contains "hype". This avoids
    22→  redundant API calls for progressive typing.
    23→
    24→Per user decision: ToolUniverse SDK for all 6 terminology systems (UMLS,
    25→SNOMED, RxNorm, ICD-10, LOINC, HPO) — single SDK, single dependency.
    26→
    27→See 40-RESEARCH.md for tool names, response formats, and latency info.
    28→"""
    29→
    30→from __future__ import annotations
    31→
    32→import logging
    33→import re
    34→from functools import lru_cache
    35→from typing import Any
    36→
    37→import pybreaker
    38→from cachetools import TTLCache  # type: ignore[import-untyped]
    39→from shared.resilience import tu_breaker
    40→from tenacity import (
    41→    before_sleep_log,
    42→    retry,
    43→    retry_if_exception_type,
    44→    stop_after_attempt,
    45→    wait_exponential,
    46→)
    47→from tooluniverse import ToolUniverse  # type: ignore[import-untyped]
    48→
    49→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import GroundingCandidate
    50→
    51→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    52→
    53→
    54→class ToolUniverseError(Exception):
    55→    """ToolUniverse returned an error dict response."""
    56→
    57→    pass
    58→
    59→
    60→class ToolUniverseUnavailableError(ToolUniverseError):
    61→    """Transient 502/503 error — eligible for retry."""
    62→
    63→    pass
    64→
    65→
    66→# Sentence extraction thresholds
    67→_SENTENCE_WORD_THRESHOLD = 4
    68→_SENTENCE_CHAR_THRESHOLD = 40
    69→
    70→# Regex to strip clinical preamble from sentence-length queries before search.
    71→# Matches common patterns like "patients must have", "history of", "diagnosis of", etc.
    72→_PREAMBLE_PATTERN = re.compile(
    73→    r"^(?:patient(?:s)?\s+(?:must|should|may|with|who|have|has|had|are|is)\s+"
    74→    r"|(?:has|have|had|is|are|must|should|may)\s+(?:a|an|the|confirmed|known|documented|active|history\s+of)\s+"
    75→    r"|(?:history\s+of|presence\s+of|absence\s+of|diagnosis\s+of)\s+)",
    76→    re.IGNORECASE,
    77→)
    78→
    79→
    80→def _extract_term_from_sentence(query: str) -> str:
    81→    """Extract medical term from sentence-length query.
    82→
    83→    Sentence-length queries (>= 4 words or >= 40 chars) are pre-processed
    84→    to remove clinical preamble before searching. This prevents 422 errors
    85→    from APIs that don't accept full sentences as search terms.
    86→
    87→    Args:
    88→        query: Raw query string from user or pipeline.
    89→
    90→    Returns:
    91→        Extracted medical term, or original query if it's short.
    92→    """
    93→    stripped = query.strip()
    94→    word_count = len(stripped.split())
    95→    too_short = (
    96→        word_count < _SENTENCE_WORD_THRESHOLD
    97→        or len(stripped) < _SENTENCE_CHAR_THRESHOLD
    98→    )
    99→    if too_short:
   100→        return stripped
   101→    cleaned = _PREAMBLE_PATTERN.sub("", stripped).strip()
   102→    if cleaned and cleaned != stripped and len(cleaned) >= 3:
   103→        logger.debug("Extracted term '%s' from sentence '%s'", cleaned, stripped[:60])
   104→        return cleaned
   105→    # Fallback: take last 4 words as the medical term
   106→    words = stripped.split()
   107→    return " ".join(words[-min(4, len(words)) :])
   108→
   109→
   110→# In-memory result cache: (tool_name, normalized_query, max_results) → candidates
   111→# TTL = 300s (5 minutes) — appropriate for autocomplete endpoints
   112→_CACHE: TTLCache = TTLCache(maxsize=1000, ttl=300)
   113→
   114→# Tool name per system (verified 2026-02-17 with ToolUniverse 1.0.18).
   115→# Single source of truth: only these tools are loaded via include_tools.
   116→_SYSTEM_TOOL_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
   117→    "umls": "umls_search_concepts",
   118→    "snomed": "snomed_search_concepts",
   119→    "icd10": "ICD10_search_codes",
   120→    "loinc": "LOINC_search_tests",
   121→    "rxnorm": "RxNorm_get_drug_names",
   122→    "hpo": "HPO_search_terms",
   123→}
   124→
   125→# Scoped tool list for ToolUniverse — only load what we use (6 tools vs 1495 total).
   126→_TOOLUNIVERSE_TOOLS: list[str] = list(_SYSTEM_TOOL_MAP.values())
   127→
   128→# ToolUniverse SDK resolves tool config "type" (e.g. ICD10Tool) via a lazy registry.
   129→# The package's static lazy registry omits some types (e.g. ICD10Tool), which triggers
   130→# "falling back to full discovery" and imports ALL modules. Pre-import our tool modules
   131→# so the classes are in the registry before any tool runs, avoiding full discovery.
   132→_TOOL_MODULES_FOR_SCOPE = (
   133→    "tooluniverse.icd_tool",  # ICD10Tool
   134→    "tooluniverse.umls_tool",  # UMLSRESTTool (umls + snomed)
   135→    "tooluniverse.loinc_tool",  # LOINCTool
   136→    "tooluniverse.rxnorm_tool",  # RxNormTool
   137→    "tooluniverse.hpo_tool",  # HPOTool
   138→)
   139→
   140→
   141→def _ensure_tool_classes_registered() -> None:
   142→    """Import tooluniverse tool modules so our tool types are in the registry.
   143→
   144→    Prevents 'Tool X not found in lazy registry, falling back to full discovery'
   145→    and avoids importing all 1495 tool modules.
   146→    """
   147→    import importlib
   148→
   149→    for mod in _TOOL_MODULES_FOR_SCOPE:
   150→        try:
   151→            importlib.import_module(mod)
   152→        except ImportError as e:
   153→            logger.debug("Optional tool module %s not imported: %s", mod, e)
   154→
   155→
   156→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   157→def _get_tu() -> ToolUniverse:
   158→    """Get or create singleton ToolUniverse instance.
   159→
   160→    lru_cache(maxsize=1) ensures only one ToolUniverse is created per process.
   161→    load_tools(include_tools=...) loads exactly the 6 terminology tools we use,
   162→    not whole categories (18 tools) or the full universe (1495 tools).
   163→
   164→    Returns:
   165→        Initialized ToolUniverse instance with medical terminology tools loaded.
   166→    """
   167→    _ensure_tool_classes_registered()
   168→    tu = ToolUniverse()
   169→    tu.load_tools(include_tools=_TOOLUNIVERSE_TOOLS)
   170→    logger.info(
   171→        "ToolUniverse initialized (scoped to %d tools: %s)",
   172→        len(_TOOLUNIVERSE_TOOLS),
   173→        sorted(_TOOLUNIVERSE_TOOLS),
   174→    )
   175→    return tu
   176→
   177→
   178→def _check_prefix_cache(
   179→    tool_name: str,
   180→    normalized_query: str,
   181→    max_results: int,
   182→) -> list[GroundingCandidate] | None:
   183→    """Check if a shorter (prefix) query is cached and filter its results.
   184→
   185→    For autocomplete: if the user typed "hyp" and we cached results, then
   186→    typing "hype" can reuse those results by filtering on preferred_term.
   187→
   188→    We only reuse when the cached shorter query returned fewer results than
   189→    max_results (meaning the API exhausted all matches — a longer query
   190→    can only produce a subset) OR when we can filter the cached results
   191→    to find matches containing the longer query.
   192→
   193→    Args:
   194→        tool_name: ToolUniverse tool name (cache key component).
   195→        normalized_query: Current query, stripped and lowered.
   196→        max_results: Max results requested.
   197→
   198→    Returns:
   199→        Filtered candidate list if a prefix match was found, None otherwise.
   200→    """
   201→    for (cached_tool, cached_query, cached_max), cached_candidates in list(
   202→        _CACHE.items()
   203→    ):
   204→        if cached_tool != tool_name or cached_max != max_results:
   205→            continue
   206→        # Check if cached query is a prefix of the new query
   207→        starts_with = normalized_query.startswith(cached_query)
   208→        is_prefix = starts_with and cached_query != normalized_query
   209→        if not is_prefix:
   210→            continue
   211→
   212→        # Filter cached results: keep candidates whose preferred_term
   213→        # contains the longer query (case-insensitive)
   214→        filtered = [
   215→            c for c in cached_candidates if normalized_query in c.preferred_term.lower()
   216→        ]
   217→
   218→        # If the original shorter query returned fewer than max_results,
   219→        # the API had no more matches — any longer query is a strict subset.
   220→        # Safe to return the filtered list even if empty.
   221→        if len(cached_candidates) < max_results:
   222→            return filtered
   223→
   224→        # If the shorter query returned max_results (truncated), we can still
   225→        # return filtered results if we got some. But if filtered is empty,
   226→        # we can't be sure — there might be results the API would return
   227→        # for the longer query that weren't in the truncated shorter results.
   228→        if filtered:
   229→            return filtered
   230→
   231→        # Truncated + no filtered matches → must query the API
   232→        return None
   233→
   234→    return None
   235→
   236→
   237→async def search_terminology(
   238→    system: str,
   239→    query: str,
   240→    max_results: int = 10,
   241→    use_cache: bool = True,
   242→) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:
   243→    """Search a terminology system via ToolUniverse SDK.
   244→
   245→    Routes the query to the appropriate ToolUniverse tool for the given system,
   246→    parses the response into GroundingCandidate objects, and caches results.
   247→
   248→    Args:
   249→        system: Terminology system to search. One of: umls, snomed, icd10,
   250→            loinc, rxnorm, hpo.
   251→        query: Clinical search term (e.g. "hypertension", "metformin").
   252→        max_results: Maximum number of candidates to return.
   253→        use_cache: Whether to use in-memory TTL cache (default True).
   254→            Set False for pipeline runs that need fresh results.
   255→
   256→    Returns:
   257→        List of GroundingCandidate objects. Empty list on any failure
   258→        (missing API key, network error, unknown system, empty results).
   259→    """
   260→    tool_name = _SYSTEM_TOOL_MAP.get(system)
   261→    if not tool_name:
   262→        logger.warning("Unknown terminology system requested: '%s'", system)
   263→        return []
   264→
   265→    # Pre-process sentence-length queries to extract medical terms.
   266→    # This prevents 422 errors caused by submitting full sentences to term search APIs.
   267→    query = _extract_term_from_sentence(query)
   268→
   269→    normalized_query = query.strip().lower()
   270→    cache_key = (tool_name, normalized_query, max_results)
   271→
   272→    if use_cache:
   273→        # Layer 1: exact match
   274→        if cache_key in _CACHE:
   275→            logger.debug("Cache hit (exact) for %s '%s'", system, query)
   276→            return _CACHE[cache_key]
   277→
   278→        # Layer 2: prefix match — reuse results from a shorter query
   279→        prefix_result = _check_prefix_cache(tool_name, normalized_query, max_results)
   280→        if prefix_result is not None:
   281→            logger.debug("Cache hit (prefix) for %s '%s'", system, query)
   282→            # Store the filtered result under the exact key too
   283→            _CACHE[cache_key] = prefix_result
   284→            return prefix_result
   285→
   286→    tu = _get_tu()
   287→    try:
   288→        raw = await _call_tool_with_resilience(
   289→            tu, system, tool_name, query, max_results
   290→        )
   291→    except pybreaker.CircuitBreakerError:
   292→        logger.warning(
   293→            "Circuit breaker open for ToolUniverse — returning cached/empty for '%s'",
   294→            query,
   295→        )
   296→        # Return cached results if available (TTLCache may still have them)
   297→        if use_cache and cache_key in _CACHE:
   298→            return _CACHE[cache_key]
   299→        return []
   300→    except ToolUniverseError as e:
   301→        logger.warning("ToolUniverse error for '%s': %s", query, e)
   302→        return []
   303→    candidates = _parse_result(system, tool_name, query, raw)
   304→
   305→    if use_cache:
   306→        _CACHE[cache_key] = candidates
   307→
   308→    logger.debug(
   309→        "ToolUniverse %s for '%s': %d candidates",
   310→        tool_name,
   311→        query,
   312→        len(candidates),
   313→    )
   314→    return candidates
   315→
   316→
   317→async def _call_tool_raw(
   318→    tu: ToolUniverse,
   319→    system: str,
   320→    tool_name: str,
   321→    query: str,
   322→    max_results: int,
   323→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   324→    """Execute a single ToolUniverse tool call (raw, no retry/circuit breaker).
   325→
   326→    Constructs the correct argument dict per system's tool specification
   327→    and calls tu.run(). Returns {} on any exception.
   328→
   329→    Args:
   330→        tu: ToolUniverse singleton instance.
   331→        system: System identifier for argument construction.
   332→        tool_name: ToolUniverse tool name to call.
   333→        query: Search term.
   334→        max_results: Max results to request.
   335→
   336→    Returns:
   337→        Raw response dict from ToolUniverse, or empty dict on error.
   338→    """
   339→    args: dict[str, Any]
   340→    if system in ("umls", "snomed"):
   341→        args = {"query": query, "pageSize": min(max_results, 25)}
   342→    elif system == "icd10":
   343→        args = {"query": query, "limit": min(max_results, 100)}
   344→    elif system == "loinc":
   345→        args = {"terms": query, "max_results": min(max_results, 20)}
   346→    elif system == "rxnorm":
   347→        args = {"drug_name": query}
   348→    elif system == "hpo":
   349→        args = {"query": query, "max_results": min(max_results, 50)}
   350→    else:
   351→        logger.warning("No argument mapping for system '%s'", system)
   352→        return {}
   353→
   354→    try:
   355→        return await tu.run({"name": tool_name, "arguments": args})  # type: ignore[return-value]
   356→    except Exception:
   357→        logger.exception("ToolUniverse %s failed for query '%s'", tool_name, query)
   358→        return {}
   359→
   360→
   361→@tu_breaker
   362→@retry(
   363→    retry=retry_if_exception_type(ToolUniverseUnavailableError),
   364→    stop=stop_after_attempt(3),
   365→    wait=wait_exponential(multiplier=1, min=2, max=10),
   366→    before_sleep=before_sleep_log(logger, logging.WARNING),
   367→    reraise=True,
   368→)
   369→async def _call_tool_with_resilience(
   370→    tu: ToolUniverse,
   371→    system: str,
   372→    tool_name: str,
   373→    query: str,
   374→    max_results: int,
   375→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   376→    """Call ToolUniverse with retry on transient errors and circuit breaker.
   377→
   378→    Decorator order: @tu_breaker (outer) wraps @retry (inner). This means
   379→    tenacity retries up to 3 times before counting as 1 circuit breaker failure.
   380→
   381→    ToolUniverse SDK catches HTTP exceptions internally and returns error dicts
   382→    instead of raising. This function detects those error dicts and converts
   383→    them to typed exceptions for retry/breaker interception.
   384→
   385→    Args:
   386→        tu: ToolUniverse singleton instance.
   387→        system: System identifier for argument construction.
   388→        tool_name: ToolUniverse tool name to call.
   389→        query: Search term (already extracted from sentence if needed).
   390→        max_results: Max results to request.
   391→
   392→    Returns:
   393→        Raw response dict from ToolUniverse.
   394→
   395→    Raises:
   396→        ToolUniverseUnavailableError: On 502/503/timeout — triggers retry.
   397→        ToolUniverseError: On other non-transient errors — no retry.
   398→    """
   399→    raw = await _call_tool_raw(tu, system, tool_name, query, max_results)
   400→
   401→    # Detect error dict: ToolUniverse catches HTTP exceptions internally
   402→    # and returns {"error": "..."} dicts instead of raising.
   403→    if isinstance(raw, dict) and "error" in raw:
   404→        error_msg = str(raw["error"])
   405→        transient_markers = ("502", "503", "Service Unavailable", "timeout", "Timeout")
   406→        if any(s in error_msg for s in transient_markers):
   407→            raise ToolUniverseUnavailableError(f"Transient error: {error_msg}")
   408→        raise ToolUniverseError(f"Non-transient error: {error_msg}")
   409→
   410→    return raw
   411→
   412→
   413→def _parse_result(  # noqa: C901
   414→    system: str,
   415→    tool_name: str,
   416→    query: str,
   417→    raw: dict[str, Any],
   418→) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:
   419→    """Parse ToolUniverse response into GroundingCandidate list.
   420→
   421→    Each system returns a different response format. This function handles
   422→    all formats with graceful degradation on unexpected shapes.
   423→
   424→    Args:
   425→        system: System identifier for format dispatch.
   426→        tool_name: ToolUniverse tool name (for error logging).
   427→        query: Original query (used as fallback for preferred_term).
   428→        raw: Raw ToolUniverse response dict.
   429→
   430→    Returns:
   431→        List of GroundingCandidate objects. Empty on error or no results.
   432→    """
   433→    if not raw:
   434→        return []
   435→
   436→    if "error" in raw:
   437→        logger.warning(
   438→            "ToolUniverse %s returned error: %s",
   439→            tool_name,
   440→            raw["error"],
   441→        )
   442→        return []
   443→
   444→    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate] = []
   445→
   446→    if system in ("umls", "snomed"):
   447→        # UMLSRESTTool: data.result.results[].{ui, name, rootSource}
   448→        # Note: snomed_search_concepts returns UMLS CUIs (not SNOMED codes)
   449→        # rootSource="SNOMEDCT_US" confirms SNOMED provenance.
   450→        results = raw.get("data", {}).get("result", {}).get("results", [])
   451→        for i, r in enumerate(results):
   452→            code = r.get("ui", "")
   453→            if not code or code == "NONE":
   454→                continue
   455→            candidates.append(
   456→                GroundingCandidate(
   457→                    source_api=system,
   458→                    code=code,
   459→                    preferred_term=r.get("name", query),
   460→                    semantic_type=r.get("rootSource"),
   461→                    score=max(1.0 - i * 0.1, 0.5),
   462→                )
   463→            )
   464→
   465→    elif system == "icd10":
   466→        # ICD10Tool: data.results[].{code, name}
   467→        results = raw.get("data", {}).get("results", [])
   468→        for i, r in enumerate(results):
   469→            code = r.get("code", "")
   470→            if not code:
   471→                continue
   472→            candidates.append(
   473→                GroundingCandidate(
   474→                    source_api="icd10",
   475→                    code=code,
   476→                    preferred_term=r.get("name", query),
   477→                    semantic_type=None,
   478→                    score=max(1.0 - i * 0.1, 0.5),
   479→                )
   480→            )
   481→
   482→    elif system == "loinc":
   483→        # LOINCTool: results[].{code/LOINC_NUM, LONG_COMMON_NAME}
   484→        results = raw.get("results", [])
   485→        for i, r in enumerate(results):
   486→            code = r.get("code") or r.get("LOINC_NUM", "")
   487→            if not code:
   488→                continue
   489→            candidates.append(
   490→                GroundingCandidate(
   491→                    source_api="loinc",
   492→                    code=code,
   493→                    preferred_term=r.get("LONG_COMMON_NAME", query),
   494→                    semantic_type=None,
   495→                    score=max(1.0 - i * 0.1, 0.5),
   496→                )
   497→            )
   498→
   499→    elif system == "rxnorm":
   500→        # RxNormTool: single result dict {rxcui, drug_name, names}
   501→        # Returns ONE best match, not a list.
   502→        # RxNorm focuses on specific drug names, not drug classes.
   503→        # Drug class terms (e.g., "beta-blockers", "ACE inhibitors") may return
   504→        # no RXCUI — the TerminologyRouter falls through to UMLS for these.
   505→        rxcui = raw.get("rxcui", "")
   506→        if rxcui:
   507→            candidates.append(
   508→                GroundingCandidate(
   509→                    source_api="rxnorm",
   510→                    code=rxcui,
   511→                    preferred_term=raw.get("drug_name", query),
   512→                    semantic_type=None,
   513→                    score=0.9,
   514→                )
   515→            )
   516→
   517→    elif system == "hpo":
   518→        # HPOTool: data[].{id, name, definition}
   519→        results = raw.get("data", [])
   520→        for i, r in enumerate(results):
   521→            code = r.get("id", "")
   522→            if not code:
   523→                continue
   524→            candidates.append(
   525→                GroundingCandidate(
   526→                    source_api="hpo",
   527→                    code=code,
   528→                    preferred_term=r.get("name", query),
   529→                    semantic_type=None,
   530→                    score=max(1.0 - i * 0.1, 0.5),
   531→                )
   532→            )
   533→
   534→    return candidates
   535→
```

```
     1→"""PDF fetcher tool: download PDF bytes from GCS or local storage.
     2→
     3→Pure function — no state dependency. Sends raw PDF bytes to Gemini
     4→via the File API (multimodal approach, no markdown conversion needed).
     5→
     6→Architecture note: This module is self-contained within protocol-processor-service
     7→and has NO dependency on api-service. Cross-service imports are reserved for
     8→workflow nodes that are integration glue.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→import os
    15→from pathlib import Path
    16→
    17→from shared.resilience import gcs_breaker
    18→from tenacity import (
    19→    before_sleep_log,
    20→    retry,
    21→    retry_if_exception_type,
    22→    stop_after_attempt,
    23→    wait_random_exponential,
    24→)
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→
    29→@gcs_breaker
    30→@retry(
    31→    retry=retry_if_exception_type(Exception),
    32→    stop=stop_after_attempt(3),
    33→    wait=wait_random_exponential(multiplier=1, min=2, max=10),
    34→    before_sleep=before_sleep_log(logger, logging.WARNING),
    35→    reraise=True,
    36→)
    37→def _download_from_gcs(bucket_name: str, blob_name: str) -> bytes:
    38→    """Download PDF bytes from GCS with retry and circuit breaker.
    39→
    40→    Args:
    41→        bucket_name: GCS bucket name.
    42→        blob_name: Blob path within the bucket.
    43→
    44→    Returns:
    45→        Raw PDF bytes.
    46→    """
    47→    from google.cloud import storage  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    48→
    49→    client = storage.Client()
    50→    bucket = client.bucket(bucket_name)
    51→    blob = bucket.blob(blob_name)
    52→    return blob.download_as_bytes()
    53→
    54→
    55→async def fetch_pdf_bytes(file_uri: str) -> bytes:
    56→    """Fetch PDF bytes from a file URI.
    57→
    58→    Supports two URI schemes:
    59→    - local:// : Reads from a local upload directory (for dev/test).
    60→      The path after 'local://' is the relative blob path within
    61→      LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR (env var, default 'uploads/protocols' to match api-service).
    62→    - gs:// : Downloads from Google Cloud Storage.
    63→
    64→    Args:
    65→        file_uri: URI of the PDF file (local:// or gs://).
    66→
    67→    Returns:
    68→        Raw PDF bytes.
    69→
    70→    Raises:
    71→        FileNotFoundError: If local file does not exist.
    72→        ValueError: If URI scheme is not recognized.
    73→    """
    74→    if file_uri.startswith("local://"):
    75→        blob_path = file_uri[len("local://") :]
    76→        # Must match api_service.gcs default so both services resolve the same path
    77→        upload_dir = os.environ.get("LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR", "uploads/protocols")
    78→        base = Path(upload_dir)
    79→        if not base.is_absolute():
    80→            base = base.resolve()
    81→        local_path = base / blob_path
    82→        if not local_path.exists():
    83→            raise FileNotFoundError(
    84→                f"Local PDF not found at {local_path}. "
    85→                f"Ensure LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR is set correctly (current: {upload_dir}) "
    86→                f"and the file has been uploaded. If the API runs in Docker with a "
    87→                f"volume mount, set LOCAL_UPLOAD_DIR to the mounted host path."
    88→            )
    89→        logger.info("Reading PDF from local path: %s", local_path)
    90→        return local_path.read_bytes()
    91→
    92→    if file_uri.startswith("gs://"):
    93→        path_without_scheme = file_uri[len("gs://") :]
    94→        bucket_name, _, blob_name = path_without_scheme.partition("/")
    95→        logger.info(
    96→            "Downloading PDF from GCS: bucket=%s, blob=%s",
    97→            bucket_name,
    98→            blob_name,
    99→        )
   100→        return _download_from_gcs(bucket_name, blob_name)
   101→
   102→    raise ValueError(
   103→        f"Unknown file URI scheme: {file_uri}. Expected 'local://' or 'gs://' prefix."
   104→    )
   105→
```

```
     1→"""TerminologyRouter: entity-type-aware routing to terminology APIs via ToolUniverse.
     2→
     3→Routes entities to terminology APIs based on entity type using a YAML config.
     4→All 6 systems (UMLS, SNOMED, ICD-10, LOINC, RxNorm, HPO) are accessed via the
     5→ToolUniverse SDK singleton in tooluniverse_client.py.
     6→
     7→Per user decisions:
     8→- Entity type → API mapping stored in config file (YAML), not hardcoded.
     9→- All terminology systems accessed via ToolUniverse SDK (single dependency).
    10→- On API failure: retry transient errors; continue on permanent failures.
    11→- Consent entities explicitly skipped with logging.
    12→- Demographic entities routed normally (not skipped); MedGemma handles derived
    13→  entity mapping (age → birthDate concept, gender → SNOMED gender concepts).
    14→
    15→See config/routing.yaml for the entity type → API mapping.
    16→"""
    17→
    18→from __future__ import annotations
    19→
    20→import logging
    21→from pathlib import Path
    22→
    23→import yaml
    24→from platformdirs import user_cache_dir
    25→from tenacity import (
    26→    before_sleep_log,
    27→    retry,
    28→    retry_if_exception_type,
    29→    stop_after_attempt,
    30→    wait_random_exponential,
    31→)
    32→
    33→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import GroundingCandidate
    34→
    35→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    36→
    37→# Default routing config path relative to this file
    38→_DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "config" / "routing.yaml"
    39→
    40→
    41→def _is_likely_acronym(text: str) -> bool:
    42→    """Check if text is likely a medical acronym.
    43→
    44→    Returns True if the text is short (<=6 chars), all uppercase, and
    45→    contains no spaces. No allowlist — adding UMLS redundantly for
    46→    terms like "BMI" or "AST" costs one extra API call but never breaks
    47→    anything, while maintaining an allowlist is brittle.
    48→
    49→    Pure string check — zero cost.
    50→
    51→    Args:
    52→        text: The entity text to check.
    53→
    54→    Returns:
    55→        True if the text appears to be a medical acronym.
    56→    """
    57→    stripped = text.strip()
    58→    if not stripped or " " in stripped:
    59→        return False
    60→    if len(stripped) > 6:
    61→        return False
    62→    return stripped.upper() == stripped
    63→
    64→
    65→# diskcache TTL: 7 days in seconds
    66→_CACHE_TTL = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60
    67→
    68→
    69→def _get_cache():
    70→    """Get or create the diskcache Cache instance.
    71→
    72→    Returns:
    73→        diskcache.Cache instance stored in user cache dir, or None if
    74→        diskcache is not installed.
    75→    """
    76→    try:
    77→        import diskcache
    78→
    79→        cache_dir = Path(user_cache_dir("medgemma-terminology-router"))
    80→        return diskcache.Cache(str(cache_dir))
    81→    except ImportError:
    82→        logger.debug("diskcache not installed — terminology API caching disabled.")
    83→        return None
    84→
    85→
    86→class TransientAPIError(Exception):
    87→    """Retriable API failure: rate limit (429), timeout (408), server error (5xx).
    88→
    89→    TerminologyRouter retries these automatically with exponential backoff.
    90→    """
    91→
    92→
    93→class PermanentAPIError(Exception):
    94→    """Non-retriable API failure: auth error (401/403), validation (400/422).
    95→
    96→    TerminologyRouter logs and skips these — retrying cannot fix them.
    97→    """
    98→
    99→
   100→class TerminologyRouter:
   101→    """Route entities to terminology APIs via ToolUniverse SDK.
   102→
   103→    Loads entity type → API mapping from routing.yaml. For each entity,
   104→    queries all matching APIs via the ToolUniverse singleton client and
   105→    returns a list of GroundingCandidate objects. MedGemma then selects
   106→    the best match downstream.
   107→
   108→    Implements error accumulation: individual API failures are logged and
   109→    skipped, not propagated. The entity is still grounded by remaining APIs.
   110→
   111→    Usage:
   112→        router = TerminologyRouter()
   113→        candidates = await router.route_entity("metformin", "Medication")
   114→        # candidates: [GroundingCandidate(source_api="rxnorm", ...), ...]
   115→
   116→    Attributes:
   117→        config: Parsed YAML config dict.
   118→        config_path: Path to the routing.yaml file used.
   119→    """
   120→
   121→    def __init__(self, config_path: Path | None = None) -> None:
   122→        """Initialize TerminologyRouter with routing config.
   123→
   124→        Args:
   125→            config_path: Path to routing.yaml. Defaults to config/routing.yaml
   126→                relative to this package.
   127→        """
   128→        self.config_path = config_path or _DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH
   129→        with open(self.config_path) as f:
   130→            self.config: dict = yaml.safe_load(f)
   131→
   132→    def get_apis_for_entity(self, entity_type: str) -> list[str]:
   133→        """Get list of API names to query for this entity type.
   134→
   135→        Returns an empty list for entity types with skip: true in routing config
   136→        (e.g. Consent) and for entity types not found in the routing config.
   137→        Both cases are logged.
   138→
   139→        Args:
   140→            entity_type: Entity type string (e.g. "Medication", "Condition").
   141→
   142→        Returns:
   143→            List of API names to query, or empty list if entity should be skipped.
   144→        """
   145→        routing_rules: dict = self.config.get("routing_rules", {})
   146→        rule = routing_rules.get(entity_type)
   147→
   148→        if rule is None:
   149→            logger.warning(
   150→                "Unknown entity type '%s' — not in routing config. "
   151→                "Skipping grounding. Add to config/routing.yaml to enable.",
   152→                entity_type,
   153→            )
   154→            return []
   155→
   156→        # Explicit skip (e.g. Consent: {skip: true})
   157→        if isinstance(rule, dict) and rule.get("skip"):
   158→            logger.info(
   159→                "Entity type '%s' is explicitly skipped in routing config "
   160→                "(no terminology grounding for this type).",
   161→                entity_type,
   162→            )
   163→            return []
   164→
   165→        if isinstance(rule, list):
   166→            return rule
   167→
   168→        # Unexpected rule format — treat as unknown
   169→        logger.warning(
   170→            "Unexpected routing rule format for '%s': %r. Skipping.",
   171→            entity_type,
   172→            rule,
   173→        )
   174→        return []
   175→
   176→    async def route_entity(
   177→        self, entity_text: str, entity_type: str
   178→    ) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:
   179→        """Route entity to all matching APIs and return all candidates.
   180→
   181→        Queries each API configured for the entity type via ToolUniverse SDK.
   182→        Individual API failures are logged and skipped (error accumulation).
   183→        The caller (MedGemma ground node) receives all available candidates
   184→        and selects the best match.
   185→
   186→        For likely acronyms, UMLS is always included in the API list
   187→        regardless of entity type configuration (UMLS has the broadest
   188→        abbreviation coverage via AUI atom-level synonyms).
   189→
   190→        Args:
   191→            entity_text: The entity text to look up (e.g. "metformin").
   192→            entity_type: The entity type (e.g. "Medication").
   193→
   194→        Returns:
   195→            List of GroundingCandidate objects from all queried APIs.
   196→            Empty list if entity type is skipped or all APIs fail.
   197→        """
   198→        api_names = self.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   199→
   200→        if not api_names:
   201→            return []
   202→
   203→        # Smart acronym routing: always include UMLS for acronyms
   204→        if _is_likely_acronym(entity_text) and "umls" not in api_names:
   205→            api_names = [*api_names, "umls"]
   206→            logger.info(
   207→                "Acronym detected ('%s') — adding UMLS to routing "
   208→                "for broader abbreviation coverage",
   209→                entity_text,
   210→            )
   211→
   212→        all_candidates: list[GroundingCandidate] = []
   213→
   214→        for api_name in api_names:
   215→            try:
   216→                candidates = await self._query_tooluniverse(api_name, entity_text)
   217→                all_candidates.extend(candidates)
   218→
   219→            except PermanentAPIError as e:
   220→                # Non-retriable: log and skip this API
   221→                logger.error(
   222→                    "Permanent API error from '%s' for entity '%s': %s",
   223→                    api_name,
   224→                    entity_text,
   225→                    e,
   226→                )
   227→            except Exception as e:
   228→                # Transient/unexpected: log and skip (tenacity already retried)
   229→                logger.error(
   230→                    "API '%s' failed for entity '%s' after retries: %s",
   231→                    api_name,
   232→                    entity_text,
   233→                    e,
   234→                )
   235→
   236→        return all_candidates
   237→
   238→    @retry(
   239→        retry=retry_if_exception_type(TransientAPIError),
   240→        stop=stop_after_attempt(3),
   241→        wait=wait_random_exponential(multiplier=1, min=2, max=10),
   242→        before_sleep=before_sleep_log(logger, logging.WARNING),
   243→        reraise=True,
   244→    )
   245→    async def _query_tooluniverse(
   246→        self, api_name: str, entity_text: str
   247→    ) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:
   248→        """Query terminology system via ToolUniverse client wrapper.
   249→
   250→        Uses the singleton search_terminology function which handles tool
   251→        dispatch, response parsing, and in-memory TTL caching.
   252→
   253→        Retries on TransientAPIError with exponential backoff (3 attempts max).
   254→
   255→        Args:
   256→            api_name: Terminology system name (e.g. "umls", "icd10").
   257→            entity_text: Term to search.
   258→
   259→        Returns:
   260→            List of GroundingCandidate objects from ToolUniverse.
   261→
   262→        Raises:
   263→            TransientAPIError: On retriable errors (rate limit, timeout, 5xx).
   264→        """
   265→        from protocol_processor.tools.tooluniverse_client import search_terminology
   266→
   267→        candidates = await search_terminology(api_name, entity_text, max_results=10)
   268→        return candidates
   269→
```

```
     1→"""MedGemma decision tool for best-match selection from TerminologyRouter candidates.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "MedGemma acts as decision-maker, minimum token usage."
     4→MedGemma evaluates candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and selects the best
     5→match for each entity. Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical
     6→reasoning, Gemini for structured output parsing.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import logging
    12→import os
    13→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
    14→
    15→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    16→    from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import TerminologyRouter
    17→
    18→from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, SystemMessage
    19→from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    20→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    21→
    22→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import (
    23→    EntityGroundingResult,
    24→    GroundingCandidate,
    25→)
    26→
    27→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    28→
    29→_model_loader = None
    30→_PROMPTS_DIR = None
    31→
    32→
    33→def _get_prompts_dir():
    34→    from pathlib import Path
    35→
    36→    return Path(__file__).parent.parent / "prompts"
    37→
    38→
    39→def _render_template(template_name: str, **kwargs: Any) -> str:
    40→    """Render a Jinja2 template from the prompts directory."""
    41→    from jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader
    42→
    43→    prompts_dir = _get_prompts_dir()
    44→    env = Environment(loader=FileSystemLoader(str(prompts_dir)), autoescape=False)
    45→    template = env.get_template(template_name)
    46→    return template.render(**kwargs)
    47→
    48→
    49→class AgenticReasoningResult(BaseModel):
    50→    """MedGemma's 3-question reasoning output for failed grounding retry.
    51→
    52→    Used by agentic_reasoning_loop to determine whether to skip an entity,
    53→    apply derived entity mapping, or rephrase the query for a better search.
    54→
    55→    Attributes:
    56→        should_skip: True if entity is not a valid medical criterion.
    57→        is_derived: True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept.
    58→        derived_term: Standard concept term if is_derived is True.
    59→        rephrased_query: Rephrased medical terminology query if applicable.
    60→        gemini_suggestion: Optional additional reformulation from Gemini.
    61→        reasoning: Brief explanation of the reasoning decisions.
    62→    """
    63→
    64→    should_skip: bool = Field(
    65→        default=False,
    66→        description=(
    67→            "True if entity is not a valid medical criterion "
    68→            "(e.g., consent, participation, willingness)"
    69→        ),
    70→    )
    71→    is_derived: bool = Field(
    72→        default=False,
    73→        description="True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept",
    74→    )
    75→    derived_term: str | None = Field(
    76→        default=None,
    77→        description=(
    78→            "Standard concept term if is_derived is True "
    79→            "(e.g., 'age' for 'age >= 18 years')"
    80→        ),
    81→    )
    82→    rephrased_query: str | None = Field(
    83→        default=None,
    84→        description=(
    85→            "Rephrased medical terminology query for better search "
    86→            "(e.g., 'hypertension' for 'high blood pressure')"
    87→        ),
    88→    )
    89→    gemini_suggestion: str | None = Field(
    90→        default=None,
    91→        description=("Optional reformulation suggestion from Gemini structuring step"),
    92→    )
    93→    reasoning: str = Field(
    94→        default="",
    95→        description="Brief explanation of the three-question reasoning",
    96→    )
    97→
    98→
    99→class GroundingDecision(BaseModel):
   100→    """MedGemma's decision for the best terminology match."""
   101→
   102→    selected_code: str | None = Field(
   103→        default=None,
   104→        description=(
   105→            "Selected terminology code (CUI for UMLS, SNOMED code, etc)."
   106→            " Null if no good match."
   107→        ),
   108→    )
   109→    selected_system: str | None = Field(
   110→        default=None,
   111→        description=(
   112→            "The API/system that produced the selected code (e.g. 'umls', 'snomed')."
   113→        ),
   114→    )
   115→    preferred_term: str | None = Field(
   116→        default=None,
   117→        description="Canonical preferred term for the selected code.",
   118→    )
   119→    confidence: float = Field(
   120→        ge=0.0,
   121→        le=1.0,
   122→        description=(
   123→            "Confidence score 0.0-1.0."
   124→            " 0.9-1.0=exact, 0.7-0.8=synonym, 0.5-0.6=partial, 0.0=no match."
   125→        ),
   126→    )
   127→    reasoning: str = Field(
   128→        default="",
   129→        description="Brief explanation for selection.",
   130→    )
   131→
   132→
   133→def _get_medgemma_model() -> Any:
   134→    """Get or create MedGemma model instance."""
   135→    global _model_loader  # noqa: PLW0603
   136→    if _model_loader is None:
   137→        from inference.config import AgentConfig
   138→        from inference.model_garden import create_model_loader
   139→
   140→        _model_loader = create_model_loader(AgentConfig.from_env())
   141→    return _model_loader()
   142→
   143→
   144→async def _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> GroundingDecision:
   145→    """Structure raw MedGemma output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   146→
   147→    Args:
   148→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma output (free-form medical reasoning).
   149→
   150→    Returns:
   151→        GroundingDecision with selected code and confidence.
   152→    """
   153→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   154→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   155→
   156→    if not google_api_key:
   157→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   158→
   159→    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   160→
   161→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   162→        model=gemini_model_name,
   163→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   164→    )
   165→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(GroundingDecision)
   166→
   167→    prompt = (
   168→        "Extract the grounding decision from this medical terminology analysis."
   169→        " Return the selected code, system, preferred term, confidence,"
   170→        f" and reasoning.\n\n{raw_text}"
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    with llm_span("gemini_structure_decision", gemini_model_name) as llm:
   174→        llm.set_request(prompt)
   175→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   176→        resp_text = str(result) if not isinstance(result, dict) else str(result)
   177→        llm.set_response(resp_text)
   178→
   179→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   180→        return GroundingDecision.model_validate(result)
   181→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   182→
   183→
   184→async def medgemma_decide(
   185→    entity: dict,
   186→    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate],
   187→    criterion_context: str,
   188→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
   189→    """Use MedGemma to select the best terminology match for an entity.
   190→
   191→    MedGemma evaluates the candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and
   192→    selects the most appropriate code for the entity. Returns a result with
   193→    confidence=0.0 and no code if no candidates are available or none are
   194→    appropriate.
   195→
   196→    Args:
   197→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   198→        candidates: List of GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   199→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   200→
   201→    Returns:
   202→        EntityGroundingResult with the selected code, confidence, and reasoning.
   203→    """
   204→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   205→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   206→    criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   207→
   208→    # If no candidates, return result with confidence=0.0
   209→    if not candidates:
   210→        logger.info(
   211→            "No candidates for entity '%s' (type=%s, criterion=%s)"
   212→            " — returning empty grounding",
   213→            entity_text[:50],
   214→            entity_type,
   215→            criterion_id[:12],
   216→        )
   217→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   218→            entity_text=entity_text,
   219→            entity_type=entity_type,
   220→            selected_code=None,
   221→            selected_system=None,
   222→            preferred_term=None,
   223→            confidence=0.0,
   224→            candidates=[],
   225→            reasoning="No terminology candidates available from any API",
   226→        )
   227→
   228→    try:
   229→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   230→
   231→        system_prompt = _render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   232→        evaluate_prompt = _render_template(
   233→            "grounding_evaluate.jinja2",
   234→            entity_text=entity_text,
   235→            entity_type=entity_type,
   236→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   237→            candidates=candidates,
   238→        )
   239→
   240→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   241→
   242→        model_name = getattr(model, "model_name", "") or getattr(model, "model", "")
   243→        messages = [
   244→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   245→            HumanMessage(content=evaluate_prompt),
   246→        ]
   247→
   248→        with llm_span("medgemma_evaluate", str(model_name)) as llm:
   249→            llm.set_request(f"[system] {system_prompt}\n\n[user] {evaluate_prompt}")
   250→            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   251→            raw_text = raw_response.content
   252→            usage: dict[str, int] = {}
   253→            um = getattr(raw_response, "usage_metadata", None)
   254→            if isinstance(um, dict):
   255→                if um.get("input_tokens"):
   256→                    usage["input_tokens"] = um["input_tokens"]
   257→                if um.get("output_tokens"):
   258→                    usage["output_tokens"] = um["output_tokens"]
   259→                if um.get("total_tokens"):
   260→                    usage["total_tokens"] = um["total_tokens"]
   261→            llm.set_response(str(raw_text), usage or None)
   262→
   263→        logger.debug(
   264→            "MedGemma evaluate response for '%s' (first 200 chars): %s",
   265→            entity_text[:30],
   266→            raw_text[:200],
   267→        )
   268→
   269→        decision = await _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text)
   270→
   271→        logger.info(
   272→            "Grounding decision for '%s': code=%s, system=%s, conf=%.2f",
   273→            entity_text[:50],
   274→            decision.selected_code,
   275→            decision.selected_system,
   276→            decision.confidence,
   277→        )
   278→
   279→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   280→            entity_text=entity_text,
   281→            entity_type=entity_type,
   282→            selected_code=decision.selected_code,
   283→            selected_system=decision.selected_system,
   284→            preferred_term=decision.preferred_term,
   285→            confidence=decision.confidence,
   286→            candidates=candidates,
   287→            reasoning=decision.reasoning,
   288→        )
   289→
   290→    except Exception as e:
   291→        logger.error(
   292→            "MedGemma decision failed for entity '%s': %s",
   293→            entity_text[:50],
   294→            e,
   295→            exc_info=True,
   296→        )
   297→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   298→            entity_text=entity_text,
   299→            entity_type=entity_type,
   300→            selected_code=None,
   301→            selected_system=None,
   302→            preferred_term=None,
   303→            confidence=0.0,
   304→            candidates=candidates,
   305→            reasoning=f"MedGemma decision failed: {e}",
   306→        )
   307→
   308→
   309→async def _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   310→    """Structure raw MedGemma reasoning output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   311→
   312→    Gemini also acts as a collaborating agent here — it can add its own
   313→    reformulation suggestions via the gemini_suggestion field when structuring
   314→    the output.
   315→
   316→    Args:
   317→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma reasoning output (free-form text).
   318→
   319→    Returns:
   320→        AgenticReasoningResult with structured 3-question answers.
   321→    """
   322→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   323→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   324→
   325→    if not google_api_key:
   326→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   327→
   328→    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   329→
   330→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   331→        model=gemini_model_name,
   332→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   333→    )
   334→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(AgenticReasoningResult)
   335→
   336→    prompt = (
   337→        "Extract the structured reasoning from this medical entity analysis. "
   338→        "Determine: (1) should_skip if entity is not a valid medical criterion, "
   339→        "(2) is_derived and derived_term if entity maps to a standard concept, "
   340→        "(3) rephrased_query if a better medical term would improve search. "
   341→        "You may also add a gemini_suggestion with your own reformulation if "
   342→        "you can improve on the analysis.\n\n"
   343→        f"{raw_text}"
   344→    )
   345→
   346→    with llm_span("gemini_structure_reasoning", gemini_model_name) as llm:
   347→        llm.set_request(prompt)
   348→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   349→        llm.set_response(str(result))
   350→
   351→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   352→        return AgenticReasoningResult.model_validate(result)
   353→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   354→
   355→
   356→async def agentic_reasoning_loop(
   357→    entity: dict,
   358→    criterion_context: str,
   359→    router: "TerminologyRouter",
   360→    attempt: int = 1,
   361→) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   362→    """Ask MedGemma 3 reasoning questions to determine retry strategy.
   363→
   364→    Called by ground_node when an entity fails initial grounding (zero
   365→    candidates or confidence < 0.5). Asks MedGemma in a single prompt:
   366→    - Q1: Is this a valid medical criterion (or should it be skipped)?
   367→    - Q2: Is this a derived entity that maps to a standard concept?
   368→    - Q3: Can this entity be rephrased for better terminology search?
   369→
   370→    Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical reasoning, Gemini
   371→    for structured output parsing (and optional reformulation suggestion).
   372→
   373→    Args:
   374→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   375→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   376→        router: TerminologyRouter instance (for get_apis_for_entity context).
   377→        attempt: Current attempt number (1-3) for prompt context.
   378→
   379→    Returns:
   380→        AgenticReasoningResult with should_skip, is_derived, derived_term,
   381→        rephrased_query, gemini_suggestion, and reasoning fields.
   382→        On error, returns default result (no skip, no rephrase) to allow
   383→        the retry loop to continue with the original query.
   384→    """
   385→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   386→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   387→    previous_query = entity.get("_previous_query", entity_text)
   388→
   389→    logger.info(
   390→        "Agentic reasoning (attempt %d) for entity '%s' (type=%s)",
   391→        attempt,
   392→        entity_text[:50],
   393→        entity_type,
   394→    )
   395→
   396→    try:
   397→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   398→
   399→        system_prompt = _render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   400→        reasoning_prompt = _render_template(
   401→            "grounding_reasoning.jinja2",
   402→            entity_text=entity_text,
   403→            entity_type=entity_type,
   404→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   405→            previous_query=previous_query,
   406→            attempt=attempt,
   407→        )
   408→
   409→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   410→
   411→        model_name = getattr(model, "model_name", "") or getattr(model, "model", "")
   412→        messages = [
   413→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   414→            HumanMessage(content=reasoning_prompt),
   415→        ]
   416→
   417→        with llm_span("medgemma_reasoning", str(model_name)) as llm:
   418→            llm.set_request(f"[system] {system_prompt}\n\n[user] {reasoning_prompt}")
   419→            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   420→            raw_text = raw_response.content
   421→            usage_r: dict[str, int] = {}
   422→            um_r = getattr(raw_response, "usage_metadata", None)
   423→            if isinstance(um_r, dict):
   424→                if um_r.get("input_tokens"):
   425→                    usage_r["input_tokens"] = um_r["input_tokens"]
   426→                if um_r.get("output_tokens"):
   427→                    usage_r["output_tokens"] = um_r["output_tokens"]
   428→                if um_r.get("total_tokens"):
   429→                    usage_r["total_tokens"] = um_r["total_tokens"]
   430→            llm.set_response(str(raw_text), usage_r or None)
   431→
   432→        logger.debug(
   433→            "MedGemma reasoning response for '%s' (first 300 chars): %s",
   434→            entity_text[:30],
   435→            raw_text[:300],
   436→        )
   437→
   438→        result = await _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text)
   439→
   440→        logger.info(
   441→            "Agentic reasoning result for '%s': skip=%s, derived=%s, "
   442→            "derived_term=%s, rephrased=%s",
   443→            entity_text[:50],
   444→            result.should_skip,
   445→            result.is_derived,
   446→            result.derived_term,
   447→            result.rephrased_query,
   448→        )
   449→
   450→        return result
   451→
   452→    except Exception as e:
   453→        logger.error(
   454→            "Agentic reasoning loop failed for entity '%s': %s",
   455→            entity_text[:50],
   456→            e,
   457→            exc_info=True,
   458→        )
   459→        # Return default result (no skip, no rephrase) — retry with original query
   460→        return AgenticReasoningResult(
   461→            should_skip=False,
   462→            is_derived=False,
   463→            derived_term=None,
   464→            rephrased_query=None,
   465→            reasoning=f"Reasoning loop failed: {e}",
   466→        )
   467→
```

> AGENT

The `_download_from_gcs` function returns `bytes` in its signature, but since google.cloud is untyped, the `blob.download_as_bytes()` returns `Any` which propagates. Since I already typed the internal variable as `data: bytes`, the function itself should return `bytes`. The issue is that with `import-untyped` suppressed, the function's return should now be properly typed. Let me check what the actual error is:

The error is that `_download_from_gcs` itself now returns `Any` because the decorator `@gcs_breaker` wraps it and the wrapper may not preserve return types properly. Let me add an explicit type annotation:

> TOOL

```json
  "old_string": "        return _download_from_gcs(bucket_name, blob_name)",
  "new_string": "        gcs_data: bytes = _download_from_gcs(bucket_name, blob_name)\n        return gcs_data"
}
```

```
-from typing import Literal, cast
+from typing import Any, Literal, cast
 
 from jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader
 from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
@@ -77,19 +77,54 @@ def _render_decompose_prompt(criterion_text: str, category: str | None) -> str:
     return template.render(criterion_text=criterion_text, category=category or "")
 
 
+def _rephrase_for_retry(text: str, attempt: int) -> str:
+    """Apply small wording changes for retry attempts.
+
+    Each attempt surfaces the same medical content with slightly
+    different phrasing so the LLM gets a fresh chance to extract entities.
+    """
+    if attempt == 1:
+        return f"Eligibility criterion: {text}. Identify every medical concept."
+    # attempt == 2
+    return f"Clinical trial requirement — {text}. What medical entities are mentioned?"
+
+
+async def _invoke_decompose(
+    structured: Any,
+    prompt: str,
+    label: str,
+    model_name: str,
+) -> DecomposedEntityList:
+    """Single invocation of the structured LLM with tracing."""
+    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
+
+    with llm_span(label, model_name) as llm:
+        llm.set_request(prompt)
+        result = await structured.ainvoke(prompt)
+        llm.set_response(str(result))
+
+    if isinstance(result, dict):
+        result = DecomposedEntityList.model_validate(result)
+    return cast(DecomposedEntityList, result)
+
+
 async def decompose_entities_from_criterion(
     criterion_text: str,
     category: str | None,
-) -> list[dict]:
+    *,
+    max_retries: int = 2,
+) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
     """Extract discrete medical entities from a criterion sentence.
 
     Uses Gemini with structured output to decompose a criterion sentence
-    into individual medical terms with correct entity types. Falls back
-    to empty list on failure -- caller handles fallback to full-text entity.
```
